Outbound Campaigns
Overview
Outbound Campaigns
enables automated outbound dialing from a centrally managed contact list. A Campaign defines the list, dialing strategy and outcome labels. Multiple human agents or an AI Voice Agent are assigned to it, and the system handles call distribution automatically. Every attempt and outcome is tracked per contact.
Outbound Campaigns is a distinct resource from
Dialer Campaign
, which covers the Power Dialer feature. A Dialer Campaign is agent-owned and manages a personal list of numbers dialled one at a time. A Campaign is admin-owned, supports multiple agents or an AI Voice Agent, and follows its own lifecycle (
draft
,
in_progress
,
paused
,
completed
,
archived
). The two resources are independent and do not share data.
API tokens have
company-wide
access to Campaigns. Since tokens are admin-scoped by nature — and admins already have full visibility in the Dashboard — all campaigns are returned with no per-agent filtering applied.
A Campaign has no overall contact limit. The synchronous
Add Campaign contacts
endpoint accepts up to 100 contacts per request. A contact import parses at most 10,000 data rows; a file with more rows fails outright, so split it across multiple imports.
Error responses
This section explains the error response shape and how clients should handle its fields; it is not an exhaustive catalogue of every error an endpoint can return. Errors from Outbound Campaigns requests follow a standard envelope format. Requests rejected upstream by the API gateway may return a shorter error response:
{
  "error": "Unprocessable Entity",
  "message": "INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION",
  "errors": [
    {
      "status": 422,
      "title": "Unprocessable Entity",
      "detail": "UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY"
    }
  ],
  "troubleshoot": "Cannot transition campaign from \"completed\" to \"in_progress\"",
  "success": false
}
Use
message
to get the specific reason. Outbound Campaigns exposes stable semantic codes you can handle in your integration:
INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION
,
CAMPAIGN_NOT_READY
,
CAMPAIGN_LAUNCH_REQUIRES_CALLER_ID_NUMBERS
,
CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION
,
OUTCOME_ALREADY_EXISTS
,
RESERVED_OUTCOME_NAME
,
OUTCOME_IN_USE
, and
BACKEND_API_UNREACHABLE
.
Use
errors[].detail
when you only need the HTTP category, such as
BAD_REQUEST
,
NOT_FOUND
,
CONFLICT
,
UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY
, or
SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE
. Validation errors, opaque
404
responses, and errors without a recognised domain code also use this status-level constant in
message
. Internal library codes are never exposed, including numeric codes.
AIVA campaigns
Human and AI Voice Agent (AIVA) Campaigns follow the same lifecycle; all sections below apply to both unless noted. Choose the type at creation.
agent_type
cannot be changed later. AIVA Campaigns have specific rules for creation and contact payloads, described below.
Create an AIVA campaign
Set
agent_type
to
aiva
in
Create a Campaign
. Any field violation returns
400
with the field name:
aiva_agent_id
is required. The field is rejected when
agent_type
is not
aiva
.
caller_id_strategy
must be
specific_number_pool
, and
caller_id_numbers
must contain at least one
number_id
from
List Campaign eligible numbers
.
calling_hours
is required and is only valid for an AIVA Campaign. It contains an IANA
timezone
and between 1 and 10
slots
. Each slot defines
from_time
,
to_time
(24-hour
HH:MM
), and
days
(
MON
–
SUN
).
from_time
must be earlier than
to_time
.
agent_type
is fixed at creation.
Update a Campaign
rejects that field; there is no endpoint for converting a human Campaign to AIVA or an AIVA Campaign to human.
Human and AIVA Campaigns have separate company entitlements. A structurally valid create request can still return
422
when AIVA Campaigns are not enabled for the company.
Add contacts to an AIVA campaign
AIVA Campaign contacts only accept
aiva_variables
. Standard fields (
first_name
,
last_name
,
email
,
company_name
,
note
) are rejected — sending any returns
422
for the whole batch. Conversely, sending
aiva_variables
to a human Campaign also fails the whole batch with
422
.
On
Add Campaign contacts
,
aiva_variables
is a flat string-to-string map and its keys cannot be empty.
Every context variable declared as
required
by the selected agent must be present and non-blank on every contact. A missing variable rejects that contact with
reason: "variable_missing"
— the rest of the batch is still queued. The response does not identify which variable is missing.
Retrieve a Campaign
returns the agent's context-variable contract, so you can discover it before building contact payloads.
aiva_variable_keys
lists the context variables the agent
requires
— the set an import's
field_mapping.variables
must cover — and
aiva_optional_variable_keys
lists the variables the agent accepts but never requires, any of which you may map. Both are
null
on a human Campaign and on an AIVA Campaign created before these fields existed;
null
means the contract is not recorded, never that the agent declares nothing.
Bulk import AIVA contacts
Use
Add Campaign contacts
for batches of up to 100 contacts. For larger batches, use the
Campaign contact import
CSV workflow.
For an AIVA Campaign,
field_mapping.variables
is required when you
create the import
. It is an ordered array of
{ context_key, source_key }
pairs and must cover every context variable the selected agent declares as required. If any required mapping is absent, the API returns
422
before creating the import and names the missing
context_key
values.
The same agent-type separation as the inline endpoint applies to CSV mappings. An AIVA import must not map
field_mapping.first_name
,
field_mapping.last_name
,
field_mapping.email
,
field_mapping.company_name
or
field_mapping.note
; a
422
names the offending fields. Conversely, a human Campaign import must not carry
field_mapping.variables
; that is also a
422
.
field_mapping.phone_number
remains required for both Campaign types because it identifies the number to dial.
The variable mapping follows these rules:
context_key: "phone_number"
is reserved and silently dropped. Map the dialled number with the required
field_mapping.phone_number
field instead.
Every
context_key
must be unique.
Every
source_key
must be unique and must not reuse the column named by
field_mapping.phone_number
.
csv_column
is a deprecated alias for
source_key
: exactly one of the two is set per entry, and existing integrations sending
csv_column
keep working.
Array order is preserved and sets the variable-column order of a later export.
If the agent's configuration cannot be read, the import is refused rather than created.
Worked example.
This CSV maps a phone column and three required agent context variables:
Phone,Customer ID,Plan,Renewal date
+33612345678,cus_1042,Pro,2026-10-01
+33698765432,cus_2084,Starter,2026-11-15
Create the import with matching column names and the variables in the order you want a later export to use:
Upload the CSV to the presigned
upload.url
from the response, following the form-field rules on
Create a Campaign contact import
. The valid rows then become contacts on the Campaign as the file is read; there is no review step and no second call. Poll the import to follow the run until it reports
imported
:
Request
GET /v1/campaigns/:id/contact_imports/:import_id
Add
"insertion_point": "start"
to the create request instead if the imported contacts should go ahead of the contacts already waiting. See
Retrieve a Campaign contact import
for the status loop and for the counts of rows an AIVA import discarded.
After contacts are queued,
Launch the Campaign
. The launch request requires the same compliance acknowledgment as a human Campaign.
Request
POST
/v1/campaigns/:id/contact_imports

{
  "file_name": "aiva-renewals.csv",
  "country_code": "FR",
  "field_mapping": {
    "phone_number": "Phone",
    "variables": [
      { "context_key": "customer_id", "source_key": "Customer ID" },
      { "context_key": "plan", "source_key": "Plan" },
      { "context_key": "renewal_date", "source_key": "Renewal date" }
    ]
  }
}
The Campaign object
The object returned by all Campaign endpoints — create, retrieve, list, update, and lifecycle operations.
Attributes
id
String
Unique identifier for the Campaign (UUID).
direct_link
String
Direct API URL.
name
String
The Campaign's name.
description
String
The Campaign's description.
Can be
null
.
status
String
The Campaign's current lifecycle status.
One of
draft
,
in_progress
,
paused
,
completed
,
archived
.
completed
cannot return to an active or editable state; its only outgoing transition is to
archived
.
archived
has no outgoing transition and cannot be deleted.
caller_id_strategy
String
How the Caller ID is chosen for outbound calls placed by this Campaign.
One of
agent_number_pool
,
specific_number_pool
.
local_presence
Boolean
Whether the Campaign uses local presence dialling (matching the contact's area code where possible).
call_attempts
Integer
Maximum number of times a contact is called before being marked unreachable.
min_interval_between_attempts
Integer
Minimum time, in seconds, between two attempts to the same contact.
external_ringing_timeout
Integer
How long, in seconds, a call rings before being considered unanswered.
campaign_outcome_time
Integer
How long, in seconds, an agent has to record a call outcome after a call ends.
end_date
String
Date the Campaign is set to stop, in UTC.
Can be
null
. Read-only: nothing in Aircall can currently set this, not even the Dashboard.
outcome_ids
Array
Which
Campaign Outcomes
agents can pick from when wrapping up a call on this Campaign.
Empty array if none are attached.
agent_type
String
Who places the calls: a human agent, or an AI Voice Agent.
One of
human
,
aiva
. Fixed when the Campaign is created - no endpoint changes it.
run_mode
String
Whether the Campaign ends on its own.
one_time
moves to
completed
once every contact is resolved;
continuous
never auto-completes and keeps dialling contacts added later until it is paused or archived.
One of
one_time
,
continuous
. Fixed when the Campaign is created - no endpoint changes it.
aiva_agent_id
String
The AI Voice Agent placing the calls.
Always
null
when
agent_type
is
human
.
calling_hours
Object
The weekly window the Campaign is allowed to dial in, evaluated in its own timezone.
Always
null
when
agent_type
is
human
. See the shape below.
created_by
Integer
Aircall user id of whoever created the Campaign, using the same ids returned by
GET /v1/users
.
Can be
null
. Always
null
when the Campaign was created through the public API, which has no user behind it.
created_at
String
Campaign's creation date, in UTC.
updated_at
String
Campaign's last update date, in UTC.
Only an AI Voice Agent Campaign has calling hours, and it cannot be launched without them. Times are wall-clock in
timezone
, so daylight saving is applied as it changes rather than baked in.
Calling hours
timezone
String
IANA timezone identifier the window is evaluated in.
For example
America/New_York
. Never a fixed UTC offset.
slots
Array
The windows themselves.
Between 1 and 10.
slots[].from_time
String
Start of the window, inclusive.
"HH:MM"
in 24h form.
slots[].to_time
String
End of the window, exclusive -
18:00
permits
17:59
and blocks
18:00
.
"HH:MM"
in 24h form, and later than
from_time
: a window cannot wrap past midnight.
slots[].days
Array
Which weekdays the window applies to.
Any of
MON
,
TUE
,
WED
,
THU
,
FRI
,
SAT
,
SUN
.
{
  "timezone": "America/New_York",
  "slots": [
    { "from_time": "09:00", "to_time": "18:00", "days": ["MON", "TUE", "WED", "THU", "FRI"] },
    { "from_time": "10:00", "to_time": "16:00", "days": ["SAT"] }
  ]
}

Set up a campaign
Create a
Campaign
, then retrieve, list, update or delete it while it is still a draft. You can also
duplicate an existing Campaign
to start from its settings without copying its contacts.
When setting up caller ID,
List Campaign eligible numbers
shows the numbers the company may use, while
List Campaign lines
reads the pool configured on a Campaign.
Create a Campaign
Create a
Campaign
in
draft
status.
A draft Campaign is not live and has no dialling side effect: nothing is charged and no calls are placed until
Launch a Campaign
is called, which is what builds the call queue.
Both kinds of Campaign can be created here: human agent (the default) and AI Voice Agent. Each is a separate per-company entitlement, so a body this endpoint accepts can still be refused with a
422
naming the one you are missing.
A Campaign using
caller_id_strategy: "specific_number_pool"
must bring at least one entry in
caller_id_numbers
, or the request is a
400
. Without numbers the Campaign could be created but never launched, so it is refused here rather than at launch. Pass the
number_id
values from
List Campaign eligible numbers
. Only ids: Aircall resolves the number itself, so an id your company cannot use for campaigns is rejected with a
400
naming it.
This endpoint does not assign Users or Teams - those are separate write operations, each documented on its own page. An AI Voice Agent Campaign needs neither: it is refused if it has any.
An AI Voice Agent Campaign, which must name its agent, dial from a specific number pool, and declare the hours it may call in:
Request
POST /v1/campaigns

{
  "name": "Q3 outreach (AI Voice Agent)",
  "agent_type": "aiva",
  "aiva_agent_id": "8f1c2c9c-9b6b-4a11-9d9f-3ab01e2e4f1a",
  "caller_id_strategy": "specific_number_pool",
  "caller_id_numbers": [12345],
  "calling_hours": {
    "timezone": "America/New_York",
    "slots": [
      { "from_time": "09:00", "to_time": "18:00", "days": ["MON", "TUE", "WED", "THU", "FRI"] }
    ]
  }
}
Body params
name
String
The Campaign's name.
Mandatory field. Cannot be blank. Max 255 characters.
description
String
The Campaign's description.
Optional. Can be
null
. Max 1000 characters.
caller_id_strategy
String
How the Caller ID is chosen for outbound calls placed by this Campaign.
Mandatory field. One of
agent_number_pool
,
specific_number_pool
.
local_presence
Boolean
Whether the Campaign uses local presence dialling.
Optional. Defaults to
false
.
call_attempts
Integer
Maximum number of times a contact is called before being marked unreachable.
Optional. Must be between 1 and 5.
min_interval_between_attempts
Integer
Minimum time, in seconds, between two attempts to the same contact.
Optional. Maximum 259200 (72 hours). The minimum floor is company-dependent (normally 3600s); a value below the applicable floor is a
400
naming the real bound.
external_ringing_timeout
Integer
How long, in seconds, a call rings before being considered unanswered.
Optional. Must be between 1 and 3600.
campaign_outcome_time
Integer
How long, in seconds, an agent has to record a call outcome after a call ends.
Optional. Must be between 1 and 3600.
outcome_ids
Array
Which outcome tags (from
List all Campaign Outcomes
) agents can pick from when wrapping up a call on this Campaign.
Optional because a Campaign can be created before any outcomes are attached. Each id must belong to your company's outcome catalogue; if any id does not, the whole request is rejected with a
404
. Omit or send an empty array for no outcomes.
caller_id_numbers
Array
The Aircall numbers this Campaign may dial out from, as
number_id
values from
List Campaign eligible numbers
.
Mandatory when
caller_id_strategy
is
specific_number_pool
, and must hold at least one id. Rejected when
caller_id_strategy
is
agent_number_pool
. Ids only - the number itself is resolved from your company's campaign-eligible set, so an id that is not in it (including another company's number) is a
400
naming the id.
List Campaign lines
reads the same ids back.
agent_type
String
Who places the calls.
Optional for backward compatibility with Campaign bodies written before AIVA support. One of
human
,
aiva
; defaults to
human
when omitted. Cannot be changed afterwards; there is no endpoint that converts a Campaign from one to the other.
run_mode
String
Whether the Campaign ends on its own once its contacts are resolved.
Optional. One of
one_time
,
continuous
; defaults to
one_time
when omitted.
continuous
never auto-completes: stop it with
Pause
, and end it with
Archive
once paused. It can also be launched with no contacts. Cannot be changed afterwards.
aiva_agent_id
String
The AI Voice Agent that will place the calls.
Mandatory when
agent_type
is
aiva
, rejected otherwise. Max 255 characters. There is no endpoint that lists AI Voice Agents, so this id has to come from your AI Voice Agent configuration.
calling_hours
Object
The weekly window this Campaign may dial in - see
Calling hours
on the Campaign object for the full shape.
Mandatory when
agent_type
is
aiva
, rejected otherwise. Required rather than optional because an AI Voice Agent Campaign cannot be launched without one, so creating it without would produce a Campaign that can never run. Between 1 and 10 slots; each slot's
from_time
must be earlier than its
to_time
, so a window cannot wrap past midnight.
The request body is validated strictly: an unknown key, or a field outside its documented range, is rejected with a
400
naming the offending field rather than being silently ignored.
status
and
end_date
are not accepted here - a new Campaign always starts in
draft
, and
end_date
cannot be set by any public or dashboard action.
code
description
400
A body field is out of range, carries an unknown key,
min_interval_between_attempts
is below the floor for your company, a
specific_number_pool
Campaign brought no
caller_id_numbers
, a
caller_id_numbers
id is not campaign-eligible for your company, or an AI Voice Agent field was combined with the wrong
agent_type
.
404
One or more
outcome_ids
do not belong to your company's outcome catalogue.
422
The
agent_type
you asked for is not enabled for your company. Human agent and AI Voice Agent Campaigns are separate per-company entitlements, so having one does not imply the other.
422
Your company has too many campaign-eligible numbers for the API to check the submitted
caller_id_numbers
ids. Set the Campaign's caller ID numbers from the Aircall Dashboard instead. This response does not mean the ids you sent are wrong.
422
The
aiva_agent_id
you sent could not be resolved for your company.
message
is
AIVA_AGENT_NOT_FOUND
. This error is not retryable; check the id.
503
The AI Voice Agent's configuration could not be read because its service was unavailable, or your company's campaign-eligible numbers could not be read. The Campaign was not created. Retryable.
Request
POST
/v1/campaigns

{
  "name": "Q3 outreach",
  "description": "Warm leads from the trade show",
  "caller_id_strategy": "agent_number_pool",
  "local_presence": true,
  "call_attempts": 3,
  "min_interval_between_attempts": 3600,
  "external_ringing_timeout": 30,
  "campaign_outcome_time": 30,
  "outcome_ids": ["9d9f3ab0-1e2e-4f1a-8f1c-2c9c9b6b1a11"]
}
Response
Status: 201 Created

{
  "campaign": {
    "id": "019f3614-18e7-7b49-aef3-93159c4ed9e3",
    "direct_link": "https://api.aircall.io/v1/campaigns/019f3614-18e7-7b49-aef3-93159c4ed9e3",
    "name": "Q3 outreach",
    "description": "Warm leads from the trade show",
    "status": "draft",
    "run_mode": "one_time",
    "caller_id_strategy": "agent_number_pool",
    "local_presence": true,
    "call_attempts": 3,
    "min_interval_between_attempts": 3600,
    "external_ringing_timeout": 30,
    "campaign_outcome_time": 30,
    "end_date": null,
    "outcome_ids": ["9d9f3ab0-1e2e-4f1a-8f1c-2c9c9b6b1a11"],
    "agent_type": "human",
    "aiva_agent_id": null,
    "calling_hours": null,
    "created_at": "2026-08-01T09:12:44.000Z",
    "updated_at": "2026-08-01T09:12:44.000Z"
  }
}

Fetch a single
Campaign
by id.

id

Unique identifier for the Campaign (UUID).
code
description
404
No Campaign with this id in your company.

/v1/campaigns/:id

    "status": "in_progress",

List all Campaigns
Fetch all
Campaigns
in your company.
This endpoint's pagination is different from the rest of the API - see the note under the response fields table below before using
page
or
current_page
, which do not apply here.
Query params
status
String
Filter by status. Comma-separated for several values.
One or more of
draft
,
in_progress
,
paused
,
completed
,
archived
. An unknown value returns a
400
, not an empty list.
name
String
Filter by name (case-insensitive, substring match).
name=Q3
matches "Q3 outreach" and "Q3 Retry" alike, not just an exact "Q3".
created_by
String
Filter by the Aircall user id of whoever created the Campaign. Comma-separated for several values.
A Campaign whose readable
created_by
is
null
never matches any filter value. This includes Campaigns created through the public API, which has no user behind it.
per_page
Integer
Number of results per page.
Default is
20
. Maximum is
50
- a higher value is not rejected, it is silently capped to 50.
cursor
String
Opaque value from a previous response's
next_cursor
, used to fetch the next page.
Treat as an opaque token: do not parse it, and do not expect it to still work after being reused out of order. Omit it to get the first page.
Meta object
count
Integer
Number of Campaigns in this page.
total
Integer
Total number of Campaigns matching the filters, across all pages.
per_page
Integer
Page size that was actually used.
next_cursor
String
Cursor to pass as
cursor
to fetch the next page.
null
on the last page.
next_page_link
String
Full URL to the next page, filters included.
null
on the last page.
This endpoint pages forward only, using a cursor rather than a page number: there is no
page
,
current_page
or
previous_page_link
, unlike other list endpoints on this API (see
Pagination
). A Campaign created while you are paging through results cannot shift already-fetched rows or duplicate one, which a page-number scheme cannot guarantee.
Request
GET
/v1/campaigns
Response
Status: 200 OK

{
  "campaigns": [
    {
      "id": "019f3614-18e7-7b49-aef3-93159c4ed9e3",
      "direct_link": "https://api.aircall.io/v1/campaigns/019f3614-18e7-7b49-aef3-93159c4ed9e3",
      "name": "Q3 outreach",
      "description": "Warm leads from the trade show",
      "status": "in_progress",
      "run_mode": "one_time",
      "caller_id_strategy": "agent_number_pool",
      "local_presence": true,
      "call_attempts": 3,
      "min_interval_between_attempts": 3600,
      "external_ringing_timeout": 30,
      "campaign_outcome_time": 30,
      "end_date": null,
      "outcome_ids": ["9d9f3ab0-1e2e-4f1a-8f1c-2c9c9b6b1a11"],
      "agent_type": "human",
      "aiva_agent_id": null,
      "calling_hours": null,
      "created_at": "2026-08-01T09:12:44.000Z",
      "updated_at": "2026-08-02T10:13:45.000Z"
    }
  ],
  "meta": {
    "count": 1,
    "total": 1,
    "per_page": 20,
    "next_cursor": null,
    "next_page_link": null
  }
}
Update a Campaign
Update a
Campaign
's editable configuration.
This is a partial update, despite the
PUT
verb. An omitted field is left untouched - it is not cleared or reset to a default. This API has no
PATCH
method, so
PUT
is reused for partial semantics rather than true replacement, which is not possible here anyway:
status
and
end_date
cannot be written by this or any endpoint.
status
is not editable here. Lifecycle changes go through the dedicated actions -
Launch a Campaign
,
Pause a Campaign
,
Resume a Campaign
,
Archive a Campaign
- each with its own preconditions and side effects.
Path params
id
String
Unique identifier for the Campaign (UUID).
Body params
name
String
The Campaign's name.
Optional. Cannot be blank if provided. Max 255 characters.
description
String
The Campaign's description.
Optional. Can be
null
to clear it.
caller_id_strategy
String
How the Caller ID is chosen for outbound calls placed by this Campaign.
Optional. One of
agent_number_pool
,
specific_number_pool
.
local_presence
Boolean
Whether the Campaign uses local presence dialling.
Optional.
call_attempts
Integer
Maximum number of times a contact is called before being marked unreachable.
Optional. Must be between 1 and 5.
min_interval_between_attempts
Integer
Minimum time, in seconds, between two attempts to the same contact.
Optional. Same floor and 259200s ceiling as on
Create a Campaign
.
external_ringing_timeout
Integer
How long, in seconds, a call rings before being considered unanswered.
Optional. Must be between 1 and 3600.
campaign_outcome_time
Integer
How long, in seconds, an agent has to record a call outcome after a call ends.
Optional. Must be between 1 and 3600.
outcome_ids
Array
Which outcome tags (from
List all Campaign Outcomes
) agents can pick from when wrapping up a call on this Campaign.
Optional. Each id must belong to your company's outcome catalogue, or the whole request is rejected with a
404
. Unlike every other field here,
[]
and omitting the field mean different things: omitting it leaves the Campaign's outcomes untouched,
[]
removes every outcome from it.
caller_id_numbers
Array
The Aircall numbers this Campaign may dial out from, as
number_id
values from
List Campaign eligible numbers
.
Optional, and
replaces
the whole pool rather than adding to it: omit the field to leave the pool alone. Ids only - the number itself is resolved from your company's campaign-eligible set, so an ineligible id is a
400
.
caller_id_strategy
is
required
whenever this field is sent - see the notice below.
calling_hours
Object
The weekly window this Campaign may dial in - see
Calling hours
on the Campaign object for the full shape.
Optional, and only on an AI Voice Agent Campaign that is still in
draft
. Send
null
to clear it. Attempting it on a human agent Campaign, or after the Campaign has left
draft
, is a
422
.
caller_id_numbers
replaces
the Campaign's pool: every number currently on it is removed and the ones you send are put in their place. To leave the pool untouched, omit the field.
The two fields travel together in both directions. Sending
caller_id_numbers
requires
caller_id_strategy
, so it is always clear which strategy the pool is for - otherwise numbers sent to a Campaign currently on
agent_number_pool
would store a pool that does nothing until someone later switched the strategy and silently activated it. Setting
caller_id_strategy
to
specific_number_pool
does
not
require you to re-send the pool if the Campaign already has one - but it is refused with a
422
if the result would be a live Campaign on that strategy with no numbers, because it would have nothing to dial from. This applies to a
draft
too, not only a live Campaign:
Create a Campaign
refuses
specific_number_pool
without numbers, so the API would otherwise contradict itself and you would only discover the problem at launch. With
agent_number_pool
the array must be empty or absent. To change neither, omit both.
On an AI Voice Agent Campaign, sending
caller_id_numbers
also triggers a fresh dialling pass over the new pool. That happens whenever the field is present, even if the numbers are identical to the ones already there, so avoid re-sending it from a periodic sync that has nothing to change.
code
description
400
A body field is out of range, carries an unknown key,
caller_id_numbers
was sent without
caller_id_strategy
, the two disagree (an empty array with
specific_number_pool
, or a non-empty one with
agent_number_pool
), or an id is not campaign-eligible for your company.
agent_type
,
aiva_agent_id
and
run_mode
are unknown keys here: each is fixed at creation, so they are rejected rather than silently ignored.
404
No Campaign with this id in your company, or one or more
outcome_ids
do not belong to your company's outcome catalogue.
409
The Campaign was modified concurrently by another request. Re-read the Campaign and retry.
message
is
CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION
.
422
The Campaign's status does not allow editing. Only
draft
,
in_progress
and
paused
are editable; a
completed
or
archived
Campaign is refused. Also returned when
calling_hours
is sent to a human agent Campaign or to an AI Voice Agent Campaign that has already left
draft
, and when the change would leave the Campaign on
specific_number_pool
with no caller ID numbers, in any status.
Request
PUT
/v1/campaigns/:id

{
  "name": "Q3 outreach - extended",
  "call_attempts": 4,
  "outcome_ids": ["9d9f3ab0-1e2e-4f1a-8f1c-2c9c9b6b1a11"]
}
Response
Status: 200 OK

{
  "campaign": {
    "id": "019f3614-18e7-7b49-aef3-93159c4ed9e3",
    "direct_link": "https://api.aircall.io/v1/campaigns/019f3614-18e7-7b49-aef3-93159c4ed9e3",
    "name": "Q3 outreach - extended",
    "description": "Warm leads from the trade show",
    "status": "in_progress",
    "run_mode": "one_time",
    "caller_id_strategy": "agent_number_pool",
    "local_presence": true,
    "call_attempts": 4,
    "min_interval_between_attempts": 3600,
    "external_ringing_timeout": 30,

    "end_date": null,

    "agent_type": "human",
    "aiva_agent_id": null,
    "calling_hours": null,
    "created_at": "2026-08-01T09:12:44.000Z",
    "updated_at": "2026-08-05T14:02:11.000Z"
  }

Delete a Campaign
Soft-delete a
Campaign
. The row is marked deleted and disappears from every read path - the same effect as deleting from the dashboard.
Only a Campaign in
draft
status can be deleted. A Campaign that has ever been launched must be moved to
archived
instead - see
Archive a Campaign
- which keeps it visible and reportable rather than removing it.

For both human and AIVA Campaigns, this public endpoint never copies contacts. It is deliberately narrower than the Dashboard's duplicate flow, which can preview and copy a filtered subset of contacts by outcome. The public endpoint only reuses the Campaign's settings as a template.

Add contacts
Add the people a
Campaign
will dial. You can

or use the
Campaign contact import
flow for a CSV.

shows who is in the Campaign.
Campaign queue items

List Campaign contacts
Fetch a
Campaign
's call queue: the contacts it dials.
?phone_number=
narrows the result to a single contact. That is the supported way to fetch one - a contact has no publicly addressable id, and within a Campaign a phone number identifies at most one contact.
Filtered or not, the response is the same paginated collection envelope - at most one element when filtered by
phone_number
. A filter that matches nothing returns an empty collection, not a
404
: the Campaign exists and was readable, the filter simply selected no rows.

next_cursor
.

List all Campaigns
- not interchangeable with a cursor from another endpoint.
phone_number
String
Narrow to the one contact with this number.
Optional. Malformed values are rejected with
400
.

phone_number

+

.

Can be

.

Can be

Can be

.

Add Campaign contacts
Add up to 100 contacts to a
Campaign
and queue them for dialling in one request. A Campaign has no overall contact limit.
This is the synchronous path, for feeding contacts in as they arrive. For larger volumes use the
Campaign contact import
flow instead, which uploads a CSV and processes it in the background.
The
contacts
array in the response is
positionally aligned
to the one you sent: the first result describes your first contact, and so on. There is no index field. Read each result's
status
and, for a rejected contact, its
reason
at the same array position as the submitted contact.
A successful response has no
success
key. HTTP
201
means at least one contact was queued. HTTP
200
can mean that every contact was rejected, so check
queued_count
,
rejected_count
and each positional result instead of treating every
2xx
as a queued batch.
Safe to retry. A replayed request queues nothing twice and changes nothing: within a Campaign a phone number identifies at most one contact, so every contact in the replay comes back as
already_in_campaign
. No idempotency key is needed.
Path params
id
String
Unique identifier for the Campaign (UUID).
Body params
country_code
String
ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 region used to read numbers written in national format, e.g.
FR
turns
06 12 34 56 78
into
33612345678
.
Required
, with no default. A wrong region does not fail loudly - it produces valid-looking numbers for another country, and the first sign of trouble is calls placed to strangers. Numbers already in full international form are unaffected. A well-formed but non-existent region such as
ZZ
is rejected with
400
.
position
String
Where the contacts land in the existing queue:
top
or
bottom
.
Optional, defaults to
bottom
.
top
puts them ahead of everyone already waiting. Note this differs from
Create a Campaign contact import
, which spells the same idea
insertion_point
with
start
/
end
.
contacts
Array
The contacts to add. Between
1
and
100
elements.
Required
. More than 100 is rejected with
400
; use the
Campaign contact import
flow for larger lists.
contacts[].phone_number
String
The number to dial. National or international format; read against
country_code
.
Required
.
contacts[].first_name
String
The contact's first name.
Optional. Human agent Campaigns only.
contacts[].last_name
String
The contact's last name.
Optional. Human agent Campaigns only.
contacts[].email
String
The contact's email address.
Optional. Human agent Campaigns only.
contacts[].company_name
String
The contact's company name.
Optional. Human agent Campaigns only.
contacts[].note
String
Free-text note to store with the contact.
Optional. Human agent Campaigns only.
contacts[].aiva_variables
Object
Context passed to the AI Voice Agent for this contact, as string keys to string values, e.g.
{"plan": "Pro"}
.
AIVA Campaigns only. Every context variable the agent declares as
required
must be present with a non-blank value. The agent's optional context-variable declarations are not required and are ignored when the Campaign validates a contact. Extra keys are accepted and passed through. Keys must be non-empty.
The accepted fields depend on the Campaign's agent type, and mixing them fails the whole request with
422
. An AIVA Campaign accepts
aiva_variables
only; a human agent Campaign accepts the five descriptive fields only. Both accept
phone_number
.
On an AIVA Campaign, a contact must carry every context variable the agent declares as
required
, each with a non-blank value. A contact missing one is reported individually as
variable_missing
rather than failing the request, so the rest of the batch can still be queued. The agent's optional context variables are not required and are ignored by Campaign validation. Extra keys are accepted and passed through to the agent.
Attributes
queued_count
Integer
How many contacts were queued for dialling.
Read this to tell a full success from a partial one - the status code only distinguishes "something was queued" from "nothing was".
rejected_count
Integer
How many were not queued.
The sum of the two always equals the number of contacts you sent.
contacts
Array
One result per submitted contact, in the same order as the request.
contacts[].status
String
queued
,
duplicate
or
invalid
.
contacts[].queue_item_id
String
Identifier of the created queue item (UUID), for use with
Campaign queue items
.
Present only when
status
is
queued
.
contacts[].phone_number
String
The normalised number, E.164 digits with no leading
+
.
Absent when
status
is
invalid
- normalising it is what failed.
contacts[].reason
String
Why the contact was not queued. See the table below.
Absent when
status
is
queued
.
Reasons
phone_number_unparseable
String
The number could not be read as a real number for the given
country_code
.
Check
country_code
before the numbers - a wrong region invalidates every row at once.
phone_number_not_dialable
String
The number is the anonymous-caller placeholder rather than a dialable number.
field_too_long
String
A descriptive field exceeded its maximum length.
first_name
and
last_name
100,
email
255,
company_name
200,
note
500. Values are rejected rather than truncated.
variable_too_long
String
An
aiva_variables
value exceeded 500 characters.
variable_missing
String
A required context variable declared by the Campaign's AIVA agent was absent or had a blank value.
The required set is fixed for as long as the Campaign can accept contacts, so this means the contact is incomplete rather than the Campaign having changed.
duplicate_in_batch
String
The same number appeared earlier in this request.
Compared after normalisation, so
06 12 34 56 78
and
+33612345678
are the same number. The first occurrence is queued.
already_in_campaign
String
The Campaign already holds this number.
Also returned when the number is already in the dial queue. This is the reason a retried request returns for every contact.
code
description
200
Nothing was queued. The response body is the same shape and carries a reason for every contact - a replayed request lands here.
201
At least one contact was queued.
400
A malformed body:
contacts
empty or longer than 100, a missing or non-existent
country_code
, an unknown field, or an empty
aiva_variables
key.
404
No Campaign with this id in your company.
422
The Campaign is
completed
or
archived
, or the submitted fields do not match its agent type.
503
The AI Voice Agent's configuration could not be read, so the Campaign's required variables could not be established. Only reachable for an AIVA Campaign whose variables were never recorded. Retry.
Request
POST
/v1/campaigns/:id/contacts

{
  "country_code": "FR",
  "position": "bottom",
  "contacts": [
    {
      "phone_number": "06 12 34 56 78",
      "first_name": "Gary",
      "last_name": "Jennings",
      "email": "gary.jennings@acme.com",
      "company_name": "Acme Corp",
      "note": "Warm lead from the trade show"
    },
    {
      "phone_number": "+33698765432"
    }
  ]
}
Response
Status: 201 Created

{
  "queued_count": 1,
  "rejected_count": 1,
  "contacts": [
    {
      "status": "queued",
      "queue_item_id": "019f3614-18e7-7b49-aef3-93159c4ed9e3",
      "phone_number": "33612345678"
    },
    {
      "status": "duplicate",
      "reason": "already_in_campaign",
      "phone_number": "33698765432"
    }
  ]
}
Remove a Campaign contact
Remove one contact from a
Campaign
's queue, so it will not be dialled. This is the undo of
Add Campaign contacts
.
The contact is addressed by its phone number, which is its identity within a Campaign - a contact has no id of its own. Feed back a
phone_number
exactly as
List Campaign contacts
returns it and it matches by construction.
Removing a contact also removes its queue item. The two are one thing from the outside: a contact is only in a Campaign because something is queued to dial it.
Removal has one of two effects.
Delete.
A contact with no recorded attempts is deleted with no call history.
Cancel next attempts.
If the contact has at least one recorded attempt, any remaining retries are cancelled and the contact leaves the queue. The latest known outcome, when one exists, is kept and made final so it remains available in the Campaign's outcomes. The API does not create a cancellation outcome.
Path params
id
String
Unique identifier for the Campaign (UUID).
phone_number
String
The contact's phone number, as returned by
List Campaign contacts
. Lenient about formatting:
33612345678
,
+33612345678
and
+33 6 12 34 56 78
all address the same contact. Percent-encode the
+
as
%2B
in the path. A national number such as
0612345678
cannot be used - it is only resolvable against a country, and an API token is company-wide with no country attached.
code
description
204
The contact was removed. With no recorded attempts, it is deleted with no call history. With at least one attempt, its remaining retries are cancelled and its latest known outcome, if any, is made final.
400
phone_number
is not an international phone number in digits. A value that is merely absent from the Campaign is a
404
, not a
400
- this code means the value could not be a phone number at all.
404
No Campaign with this id in your company, or no contact with this phone number in it. Deliberately not an idempotent
204
: a mistyped number stays distinguishable from a real removal.
Request
DELETE
/v1/campaigns/:id/contacts/:phone_number
Response
Status: 204 No Content
Campaign queue items
A
Campaign
's dial queue, in dial order:
list
it to see who's next and how the last attempt on each went,
reorder
an item to change its position, or
reschedule
one to change when it's next attempted.
List Campaign contacts
answers who is in the queue; this answers what order and when.
List Campaign queue items
Fetch a
Campaign
's dial queue, in dial order.
List Campaign contacts
answers who is in the Campaign and
Retrieve Campaign statistics
answers the aggregate; this answers what order they will be called in and how the last attempt on each went.
Deliberately narrow. Scheduler internals - retry counters, the next intended attempt time, and the derived live-queue display state - are not published here, to avoid freezing today's retry model into the contract.
Paged by cursor like every other collection on this API, but the cursor encodes queue position rather than creation time, because the queue is ordered by position. This is invisible to a client - the cursor is opaque either way - but a cursor from this endpoint is not interchangeable with one from another.
Path params
id
String
Unique identifier for the Campaign (UUID).
Query params
per_page
Integer
Number of results per page.
Default is
20
. Maximum is
50
, clamped rather than rejected.
cursor
String
Opaque value from a previous response's
next_cursor
.
See the notice above - not interchangeable with a cursor from another endpoint.
Attributes
id
String
Unique identifier for this queue entry (UUID).
phone_number
String
The number to dial - the identity of the queued row, tied to a contact by Campaign plus phone number rather than by a contact id.
position
String
Opaque lexicographic ordering key.
Compare it, do not parse it or do arithmetic on it - positions are not consecutive and gaps carry no meaning.
last_attempt_at
String
When the last attempt happened, in UTC.
Can be
null
before the first attempt.
last_attempt_id
String
The attempt behind
last_attempt_at
, joinable to the
id
field of
List Campaign call outcomes
for the typed outcome.
Can be
null
before the first attempt.
scheduled_at
String
When this entry is due, if it was queued for a specific time rather than for immediate dialling.
Can be
null
otherwise.
created_at
String
In UTC.
updated_at
String
In UTC.
code
description
400
A malformed
cursor
.
404
No Campaign with this id in your company.
Request
GET
/v1/campaigns/:id/queue_items
Response
Status: 200 OK

{
  "queue_items": [
    {
      "id": "aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa",
      "phone_number": "33987654321",
      "position": "n",
      "last_attempt_at": "2026-08-03T11:14:46.000Z",
      "last_attempt_id": "44444444-4444-4444-8444-444444444444",
      "scheduled_at": null,
      "created_at": "2026-08-01T09:14:00.000Z",
      "updated_at": "2026-08-03T11:16:25.000Z"
    }
  ],
  "meta": {
    "count": 1,
    "total": 1,
    "per_page": 20,
    "next_cursor": null,
    "next_page_link": null
  }
}
Reorder a Campaign queue item
Move one entry of a
Campaign
's dial queue (see
List Campaign queue items
) to the top or bottom, or past its immediate neighbour in either direction.
Moving an entry already at a boundary -
top
when it is already first,
bottom
when it is already last - is not an error: the entry is returned unchanged.
The queue entry must belong to the Campaign named by
id
. A wrong or nonexistent Campaign id returns the same
404
as a missing or cross-company queue entry, and nothing is reordered.
Path params
id
String
Unique identifier for the Campaign (UUID).
queue_item_id
String
Unique identifier for the queue entry (UUID).
Body params
direction
String
Where to move the entry.
Mandatory field. One of
top
,
up
,
down
,
bottom
.
last_attempt_at
and
last_attempt_id
are always
null
in this response, even for an entry with attempt history: reordering never touches attempt data, so nothing here is re-fetched. Read them from
List Campaign queue items
instead.
code
description
400
direction
is missing or not one of the accepted values.
404
No queue entry with this id under this Campaign in your company. A wrong or nonexistent Campaign id produces the same response.
409
The entry was modified concurrently by another request. Re-read and retry.
message
is
CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION
.
Request
POST
/v1/campaigns/:id/queue_items/:queue_item_id/reorder

{
  "direction": "top"
}
Response
Status: 200 OK

{
  "queue_item": {
    "id": "aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa",
    "phone_number": "33987654321",
    "position": "m",
    "last_attempt_at": null,
    "last_attempt_id": null,
    "scheduled_at": null,
    "created_at": "2026-08-01T09:14:00.000Z",
    "updated_at": "2026-08-03T11:16:25.000Z"
  }
}
Reschedule a Campaign queue item
Set, change or clear when one
Campaign
queue entry (see
List Campaign queue items
) is next due. This per-entry
scheduled_at
value is not the Campaign's retry interval:
min_interval_between_attempts
separately controls the minimum gap between attempts to the same contact.
Setting
scheduled_at
defers the entry to that time instead of immediate dialling;
null
clears it. This never changes the entry's position in the queue. A scheduled entry keeps its place and is skipped until its due time.
Path params
id
String
Unique identifier for the Campaign (UUID).
queue_item_id
String
Unique identifier for the queue entry (UUID).
Body params
scheduled_at
String
When the entry becomes due, in ISO-8601 with a UTC offset.
Mandatory field, but nullable - pass
null
to clear it back to immediate dialling. There is no "leave unchanged" option; every call overwrites it.
last_attempt_at
and
last_attempt_id
are always
null
in this response - see the equivalent notice under
Reorder a Campaign queue item
.
The queue entry must belong to the Campaign named by
id
. A wrong or nonexistent Campaign id returns the same
404
as a missing or cross-company queue entry, and nothing is rescheduled.
code
description
400
scheduled_at
is missing, or present but not a valid ISO-8601 datetime with an offset.
404
No queue entry with this id under this Campaign in your company. A wrong or nonexistent Campaign id produces the same response.
409
The entry was modified concurrently by another request. Re-read and retry.
message
is
CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION
.
Request
POST
/v1/campaigns/:id/queue_items/:queue_item_id/schedule

{
  "scheduled_at": "2026-08-04T09:00:00+02:00"
}
Response
Status: 200 OK

{
  "queue_item": {
    "id": "aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa",
    "phone_number": "33987654321",
    "position": "n",
    "last_attempt_at": null,
    "last_attempt_id": null,
    "scheduled_at": "2026-08-04T07:00:00.000Z",
    "created_at": "2026-08-01T09:14:00.000Z",
    "updated_at": "2026-08-03T11:20:03.000Z"
  }
}
Campaign contact import
The CSV workflow for loading contacts into a
Campaign
's dial queue, in two steps:
create
an import and get a presigned upload target, then
retrieve
it to follow the file as its valid rows are added to the Campaign. There is no separate call to queue the contacts, and nothing else to read: the import itself carries the whole summary.
For an AIVA Campaign, the create step must map CSV columns to every required agent context variable. See the end-to-end
AIVA Campaign bulk-import example
.
There is no review step. An uploaded file is imported directly: the valid rows become Campaign contacts and join the dial queue as they are parsed, so a wrong file queues real calls to real people. There is no undo once dialling starts - inspect the file before uploading it.
Contact import status lifecycle
Every import starts at
pending
and moves through the states below.
imported
,
failed
and
stopped
are terminal.
status
meaning
pending
The import exists and the upload target has been issued, but the CSV has not reached it. Nothing is queued yet. A file that never arrives, or arrives after
upload.expires_at
, moves the import to
failed
.
processing
The file arrived and is being read. Each batch of valid rows is added to the Campaign as it is parsed, so the contacts of a
processing
import are already in the dial queue.
imported
Every row has been read. All valid rows are Campaign contacts; invalid and duplicate rows were discarded.
failed
The import stopped on an error - an upload that never arrived, an unreadable file, or a run that lost its worker. Any contacts added before the failure stay in the queue. The response does not say which error it was.
stopped
The import was stopped from the Aircall dashboard before it finished reading the file. Contacts added before the stop stay in the queue.
A
failed
or
stopped
import is a partial import, not an undone one. The import's own counts say how far it got -
valid_count
rows are Campaign contacts,
invalid_count
and
duplicate_count
were discarded - so import only the rest.
ready
,
promoting
and
promoted
are not part of this lifecycle. An import created before CSV imports became direct still reports them rather than an invented state; nothing new is created that way.
Create a Campaign contact import
The way to load a CSV file of contacts into a
Campaign
: create the import and get somewhere to upload the file. Once the file is uploaded and read, its valid rows become contacts on the Campaign and join its dial queue - there is no review step and no second call to queue them.
Retrieve a Campaign contact import
reports the progress. A Campaign has no overall contact limit. One import parses at most 10,000 data rows; a file with more rows fails outright, so split it across multiple imports.
The file does not go through this endpoint or through
api.aircall.io
at all.
The response carries a presigned upload target; your integration uploads the CSV directly there as
multipart/form-data
, with
fields
sent exactly as returned, in order, and the file part last.
The uploaded file is imported directly. There is no review step between parsing and dialling: as each batch of rows is read, the valid ones are added to the Campaign and queued, so a wrong file puts real calls in front of real people. Check the file before uploading, not after.
One import in flight per Campaign. Creating a second one while an earlier import is still
pending
or
processing
fails with
422
- imports cannot be pipelined.
Add a separate form field literally named
Content-Type
, with the value from
upload.content_type
(currently always
text/csv
), before the file part. S3's upload policy checks this field, not the MIME
Content-Type:
header on the file part itself - omitting it fails the upload with a
403 AccessDenied
("Policy Condition failed") even though the file part's own header is correct.
An AIVA Campaign requires
field_mapping.variables
to map CSV columns to every context variable its agent declares as required. A missing required mapping returns
422
, names the missing
context_key
values, and creates no import. See the complete
AIVA bulk-import example
.
The mapping fields must match the Campaign's agent type. An AIVA import rejects
field_mapping.first_name
,
field_mapping.last_name
,
field_mapping.email
,
field_mapping.company_name
and
field_mapping.note
with
422
naming the offending fields. A human Campaign import rejects
field_mapping.variables
with
422
.
field_mapping.phone_number
is required for both.
Path params
id
String
Unique identifier for the Campaign (UUID).
Body params
file_name
String
The file's name, for your own reference.
Mandatory field. Not used to locate the object. Max 255 characters.
file_size
Integer
Declared size of the CSV file in bytes.
Mandatory field. Must be greater than
0
and no more than 1,048,576 bytes. The API validates this value before creating the import and returning an upload target.
country_code
String
ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 region used to read national-format numbers in the file, e.g.
FR
turns
06 12 34 56 78
into
33612345678
.
Mandatory field, two
uppercase
letters -
fr
is rejected with a
400
. A wrong value does not fail loudly - it produces syntactically valid numbers for the wrong country. Numbers already in full international form are unaffected.
insertion_point
String
Where the imported contacts land in the Campaign's queue.
Optional. One of
start
,
end
. Defaults to
start
, which puts them ahead of everyone already waiting;
end
appends them behind the existing queue. Read when the file is imported - it is not an option on any later call.
field_mapping
Object
Maps Campaign contact fields and agent context variables to CSV columns.
Mandatory field. Every
source_key
(or legacy
csv_column
) value must exactly match a header in the file's first row.
field_mapping.phone_number
String
The column holding the phone number.
Mandatory for both human and AIVA Campaign imports - a row without one cannot be called.
field_mapping.variables
Array
Ordered mappings from CSV columns to the agent's context variables, as
{ context_key, source_key }
pairs.
Required for an AIVA Campaign and must cover every context variable its agent declares as required. Rejected for a human Campaign. Array order is preserved for a later export.
field_mapping.variables[].context_key
String
Agent context-variable key.
Must be unique and cannot be blank. The reserved key
phone_number
is silently dropped; use
field_mapping.phone_number
for the dialled number.
field_mapping.variables[].source_key
String
CSV column mapped to this context variable.
Preferred
term.
Exactly one of
source_key
or
csv_column
per entry. Must be unique across entries, cannot be blank, and cannot reuse the column mapped by
field_mapping.phone_number
.
field_mapping.variables[].csv_column
String
CSV column mapped to this context variable.
Deprecated
alias for
source_key
- still accepted, existing integrations keep working.
Exactly one of
source_key
or
csv_column
per entry; an entry carrying both (or neither) is rejected with a
400
. Same constraints as
source_key
. Prefer
source_key
for new integrations.
field_mapping.first_name
String
The column holding the first name.
Optional - omit the key entirely to skip it. If present, cannot be blank.
field_mapping.last_name
String
The column holding the last name.
Optional - omit the key entirely to skip it. If present, cannot be blank.
field_mapping.email
String
The column holding the email address.
Optional - omit the key entirely to skip it. If present, cannot be blank.
field_mapping.company_name
String
The column holding the company name.
Optional - omit the key entirely to skip it. If present, cannot be blank.
field_mapping.note
String
The column holding a free-text note.
Optional - omit the key entirely to skip it. If present, cannot be blank.
The API refuses an AIVA import if it cannot read the selected agent's configuration. It never creates an import whose required context-variable set could not be verified.
Attributes
contact_import.id
String
Unique identifier for the import (UUID).
Use it to poll the import.
contact_import.status
String
Always
pending
here - the CSV has not been uploaded yet.
The import moves to
processing
once the upload lands. See
Retrieve a Campaign contact import
.
upload.url
String
Upload the file here, as
multipart/form-data
.
upload.fields
Object
Opaque signed policy fields.
Send all of them as form fields, in this order, before the file part. Do not reconstruct them.
upload.expires_at
String
After this the URL is refused and the import fails, in UTC.
One hour from creation. Nothing has been queued up to this point, so create the import again.
upload.max_file_size_bytes
Integer
Largest file the API and S3 will accept.
Currently 1,048,576 (1 MB). The API validates the declared
file_size
before returning an upload target and returns
422
when it is too large. S3 also enforces the declared size during upload.
upload.content_type
String
The only
Content-Type
the upload accepts.
Exact match -
text/csv; charset=utf-8
is rejected.
upload.max_rows
Integer
Largest number of data rows one import will parse.
Currently 10,000. A file with more rows fails the import outright once uploaded - split it instead.
code
description
400
A body field is missing, blank, exceeds its length,
country_code
is not a two-letter code,
insertion_point
is not
start
or
end
, or a key the body does not define is present. For
file_size
, a missing value returns
file_size is required
; the value must be a positive integer.
404
No Campaign with this id in your company.
422
The declared
file_size
exceeds the upload cap (
file_size must be at most 1048576 bytes
); an import is already
pending
or
processing
; the Campaign is
completed
or
archived
; an AIVA mapping omits required agent context variables; or the mapping fields do not match the Campaign's agent type. Missing variables and agent-type mismatches name the missing or offending fields.
500
Unexpected server error. The import was not created; retry the request.
Request
POST
/v1/campaigns/:id/contact_imports

{
  "file_name": "leads-q3.csv",
  "file_size": 24576,
  "country_code": "FR",
  "insertion_point": "start",
  "field_mapping": {
    "phone_number": "Phone",
    "first_name": "First name",
    "last_name": "Last name",
    "email": "Email",
    "company_name": "Company",
    "note": "Notes"
  }
}

    "fields": {
      "key": "contacts/12345/019.../csv/.../0199c8b0-6e2b-7c4e-9a1e-2f6b3c9d4e5f",
      "policy": "eyJleHBpcmF0aW9uIjoiMjAyNi0wOC0xMFQxNTowMjoxMVoi...",
      "x-amz-signature": "..."
    },

Retrieve a Campaign contact import
Poll this after uploading the CSV, while an import's
status
is
pending
or
processing
; stop at
imported
,
failed
or
stopped
. That loop is guaranteed to terminate - a stuck worker surfaces as
failed
rather than as
processing
forever.
This reports an import, not a queue. The import adds each valid row to the Campaign as the file is read, so a
processing
import has already queued contacts:
failed
and
stopped
do not undo them.
The full import summary is here: status, the four counts and the timestamps. There are no per-row results to fetch alongside it - an import is read and applied batch by batch and keeps only the counts.
Path params
id
String
Unique identifier for the Campaign (UUID).
import_id
String
Unique identifier for the import (UUID), from
Create a Campaign contact import
.
Attributes
id
String
Unique identifier for the import (UUID).
status
String
Where the import is in its lifecycle.
One of
pending
,
processing
,
imported
,
failed
,
stopped
- see the
status lifecycle
for what each one means.
ready
,
promoting
and
promoted
are reported only by imports created before the import became direct.
source
String
How the import was created.
csv
for an upload through this endpoint.
file_name
String
As supplied at creation.
country_code
String
As supplied at creation.
total_count
Integer
Rows read from the file so far.
0
until reading starts, then grows while the import runs. Final at
imported
.
valid_count
Integer
Rows that passed validation and were added to the Campaign.
Grows while
processing
- those contacts are already in the dial queue. Final at
imported
.
invalid_count
Integer
Rows that failed validation and were discarded, so they were never queued.
This count is the whole record of them: an import keeps counts, not per-row results.
duplicate_count
Integer
Rows dropped as duplicates - repeated within the file, or already a contact on this Campaign.
created_at
String
In UTC.
updated_at
String
In UTC.
pending
is where every import starts and where it stays until the CSV reaches the upload target. An import that never sees a file eventually fails; nothing has been queued before that point, so create the import again.
A
failed
import does not publish why it failed. Rows it had already added stay in the Campaign, and the counts above are how far it got.
code
description
404
No import with this id under this Campaign in your company.
500
Unexpected server error. The import state is unchanged; retry the request.
Request
GET
/v1/campaigns/:id/contact_imports/:import_id
Response
Status: 200 OK

{
  "contact_import": {
    "id": "0199c8b0-6e2b-7c4e-9a1e-2f6b3c9d4e5f",
    "status": "imported",
    "source": "csv",
    "file_name": "leads-q3.csv",
    "country_code": "FR",
    "total_count": 250,
    "valid_count": 231,
    "invalid_count": 12,
    "duplicate_count": 7,
    "created_at": "2026-08-10T14:02:11.000Z",
    "updated_at": "2026-08-10T14:02:19.000Z"
  }
}

and

assigned to a

.

resolves those settings into the people who will dial.
Campaign users
The Users directly assigned to a
Campaign
:
list
them,
assign
more, or
remove
one. This is the assignment setting, not the resolved set of agents who will actually be called - see
List Campaign agents
for that.
List Campaign users
Fetch the Users
directly assigned
to a
Campaign
- the assignment setting, not the resolved set of agents who will actually be called.
This is a setting, and its
POST
/
DELETE
counterparts round-trip against it: assigning a User makes them appear here, removing them makes them disappear (unless a Team assignment also places them - see the note on
Remove a user from a Campaign
). If you want everyone who will actually be called, including agents held only through a Team, use
List Campaign agents
instead.
Returns the Aircall
user_id
only - name, email and availability belong to

and are not duplicated here.

Assign users to a Campaign
Assign one or more Users to a
Campaign
.
Additive and idempotent: Users already on the Campaign stay on it, and re-posting an already-assigned
user_id
is a no-op. The whole request succeeds or none of it does - a single unresolvable id rejects the request before anything is written.
A caller who wants the resulting membership re-reads
List Campaign users
.
Path params
id
String
Unique identifier for the Campaign (UUID).
Body params
user_ids
Array<Integer>
Aircall user ids, as returned by
GET /v1/users
or
List Campaign users
.
Mandatory field. At least 1, at most 100 ids per request - submit larger assignments in batches. Duplicates are tolerated.
code
description
400
A malformed body (unknown key, non-integer,
0
, negative, string id, or more than 100 ids); a
user_id
that does not exist in your company (indistinguishable from a
user_id
belonging to another company); or the Campaign is an AI Voice Agent (AIVA) Campaign, which cannot take human agent assignments.
404
No Campaign with this id in your company.
422
The Campaign is
completed
or
archived
- assignment is only editable in
draft
,
in_progress
and
paused
.
503
Company membership could not be checked because an internal dependency was unreachable. Safe to retry.
message
is
BACKEND_API_UNREACHABLE
.
Request
POST
/v1/campaigns/:id/users

{
  "user_ids": [1, 2, 3]
}
Response
Status: 204 No Content
Remove a user from a Campaign
Remove one User's individual assignment from a
Campaign
.
user_id
is the value returned by
List Campaign users
, not that endpoint's own
id
.
A User who is on the Campaign only through an assigned Team answers
204
here while
staying on the Campaign
: this endpoint only rewrites the directly-assigned set, which never held them in the first place. Their Team membership is what keeps them there - removing that is a change to the Team, via
Remove a team from a Campaign
. Check
assigned_directly
and
team_ids
on
List Campaign agents
beforehand to know which case you are in.

List Campaign users
.
code
description

No Campaign with this id in your company; or this User is not on the Campaign at all, whether directly or through a Team - see the notice above for the case where they are on it only through a Team, which is not a
404
.

The Campaign is
completed
or
archived
- assignment is only editable in
draft
,
in_progress
and
paused
.

List Campaign agents
Fetch every agent who will actually be called on this
Campaign
, and why. This is the resolved
effect
of
List Campaign users
and
List Campaign teams
, which publish the
settings
- a direct assignment and an assigned Team are two different ways to end up in this list, and an agent can be held by both at once.
Read-only. There is no
POST
or
DELETE
here, and there never will be: this list is derived from the current assignment settings. To change who is called, change a setting on
List Campaign users
or
List Campaign teams
- this endpoint recomputes automatically. For the same reason there is no row
id
: nothing here is individually addressable.
assigned_directly
and
team_ids
are independent, not alternatives - together they answer "why is this person still being called?":
assigned_directly
team_ids
meaning
true
[]
Held by a direct assignment only.
false
[7]
Held through Team 7 only.
true
[7, 9]
Held by a direct assignment
and
two Teams.
Path params
id
String
Unique identifier for the Campaign (UUID).
Query params
per_page

Number of results per page.
Default is
20
. Maximum is
50
, clamped rather than rejected.
cursor
String
Opaque value from a previous response's
next_cursor
.
Same forward-only cursor mechanism as
List all Campaigns
- not interchangeable with a cursor from another endpoint.
Attributes
user_id
Integer
Aircall user id - see
User
.

Boolean
Whether this agent carries a direct assignment of their own.
team_ids
Array<Integer>
Assigned Teams that also place this agent in the ring.
Empty when the only justification is a direct assignment. Ascending, so the value is stable between requests.
created_at
String
In UTC.
updated_at
String
In UTC.

400
A malformed
cursor
.

List Campaign lines
Fetch a
Campaign
's caller-id pool: the Aircall numbers it is allowed to dial out from when
caller_id_strategy
is
specific_number_pool
.
An empty pool is why
Launch a Campaign
and
Resume a Campaign
answer
422
for a
specific_number_pool
Campaign: the dispatcher rotates over this pool at dial time, and launching or resuming with nothing to rotate over is refused rather than left to fail per call.

The Campaign Eligible Number object
A Campaign Eligible Number is an Aircall number in your company that is allowed to be used as the outbound caller ID for a
Campaign
- one that passes Aircall's outbound dialling compliance rules for its country and phone type.
This resource is read-only and
company-wide
, the same as
Campaign Outcome
- there is no single Campaign context here. Eligibility is evaluated per company, not per Campaign: a number excluded here is also absent from every Campaign's
List Campaign lines
pool.
Only eligible numbers are returned - there is no per-number ineligibility reason on this endpoint. A Campaign whose caller-id pool ends up empty because none of its assigned numbers are eligible refuses to
launch
or
resume
.
Attributes
number_id
Integer
Aircall number id - see
Number
.
e164
String
The number, in E.164 format.
name
String
The number's display name, as configured in the Dashboard.
country
String
ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 country code the number is eligible in.
phone_type
String
The number's phone type.
One of
geographic_local
,
national
,
mobile
,
toll_free
,
shared_costs
.
Endpoints
GET
/v1/campaign_eligible_numbers
List Campaign eligible numbers
List Campaign eligible numbers

Run the campaign
Launch
a
Campaign
to start dialling, then
pause
and
resume
it while it runs.
Retrieve Campaign statistics
reports its progress.
A Campaign with
run_mode: "one_time"
(the default) reaches
completed
automatically after its contacts are resolved. A
run_mode: "continuous"
Campaign never auto-completes: when its queue drains it stays
in_progress
and dials whatever contacts are added later, until you
pause
it, or pause and then
archive
it. It cannot return from
completed
to an active or editable state; the only available transition is to
archived
. Archive is irreversible.
Launch a Campaign
Take a
draft
Campaign
live. This builds the call queue from the Campaign's contacts and moves its status to
in_progress
- the only action that does either, which is why it is a separate endpoint from
Pause
,
Resume
and
Archive
.
A
run_mode: "one_time"
Campaign needs at least one contact to launch. A
run_mode: "continuous"
Campaign can be launched with an empty queue - it stays live and dials contacts as they are added.
Every launch requires
compliance_acknowledged: true
, for both human and AI Voice Agent Campaigns. By setting it, you attest that your company has the right to dial every contact in the Campaign and accepts responsibility for consent, do-not-call rules and calling hours. Omitting the field is a
400
; sending
false
is a
422
and does not launch the Campaign.
Path params
id
String
Unique identifier for the Campaign (UUID).
Body params
compliance_acknowledged
Boolean
Confirms that your company has the right to dial the Campaign's contacts and accepts responsibility for consent, do-not-call rules and calling hours.
Required and must be
true
. It has no default.
code
description
400
compliance_acknowledged
is missing or is not a Boolean, or the body contains an unknown field.
message
is
BAD_REQUEST
.
404
No Campaign with this id in your company.
message
is
NOT_FOUND
.
409
The Campaign was modified concurrently by another request. Re-read the Campaign and retry.
message
is
CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION
.
422
The Campaign is not in
draft
status; it is a
one_time
Campaign with no contacts yet; it uses
caller_id_strategy: "specific_number_pool"
with an empty pool (see
List Campaign lines
); it is an AIVA Campaign missing calling hours;
compliance_acknowledged
is
false
; or the Campaign's agent type is no longer enabled for your company.
message
is
CAMPAIGN_NOT_READY
for status, contacts, calling hours or acknowledgment;
CAMPAIGN_LAUNCH_REQUIRES_CALLER_ID_NUMBERS
for an empty specific-number pool; and
UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY
for the entitlement check.
Request
POST
/v1/campaigns/:id/launch

{
  "compliance_acknowledged": true
}
Response
Status: 200 OK

{
  "campaign": {
    "id": "019f3614-18e7-7b49-aef3-93159c4ed9e3",
    "direct_link": "https://api.aircall.io/v1/campaigns/019f3614-18e7-7b49-aef3-93159c4ed9e3",
    "name": "Q3 outreach",
    "description": "Warm leads from the trade show",
    "status": "in_progress",
    "run_mode": "one_time",
    "caller_id_strategy": "agent_number_pool",
    "local_presence": true,
    "call_attempts": 3,

  }

Pause a Campaign
Move an
in_progress
Campaign
to
paused
. Dialling stops for new attempts; a call already in flight is allowed to finish first.
Pausing ends every agent's active session on this Campaign and notifies Pulse, so they stop being shown as on-campaign. An agent with a call still in progress at the moment of pausing is handled once that call ends, rather than being cut off.

String
Unique identifier for the Campaign (UUID).
code
description

No Campaign with this id in your company.

is
NOT_FOUND
.

The Campaign was modified concurrently by another request. Re-read the Campaign and retry.
message
is
CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION
.
422
The Campaign is not in
in_progress
status. Only that status can be paused.
message
is
INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION
.

Resume a Campaign
Move a
paused
Campaign
back to
in_progress
. Named "resume" rather than "start": a
draft
Campaign reaches
in_progress
through
Launch a Campaign
instead, which also builds the call queue - routing a
draft
Campaign through this endpoint would change its status without ever queueing a contact.
Unlike
Pause
, resuming has no agent-session cleanup to perform. It does re-check that a Campaign using
caller_id_strategy: "specific_number_pool"
still has numbers in its pool. A pool emptied while the Campaign was paused fails here rather than producing calls with no caller ID.

is
CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION
.
422
The Campaign is not in
paused
status (
message: INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION
); or it uses
caller_id_strategy: "specific_number_pool"
and its pool is now empty (
message: CAMPAIGN_LAUNCH_REQUIRES_CALLER_ID_NUMBERS
; see
List Campaign lines
).

Archive a Campaign
Move a
Campaign
to
archived
. Unlike
Delete a Campaign
, an archived Campaign stays visible and reportable - archiving is a lifecycle state, not a removal.
Archiving is irreversible.
An archived Campaign has no outgoing transition and can never be deleted, resumed, relaunched or returned to another state. Delete a draft before archiving it if you need to remove it. Use
Pause
instead if the Campaign may run again.
Only a Campaign in
draft
,
paused
or
completed
status can be archived. An
in_progress
Campaign must be paused first. A
completed
Campaign cannot return to an active or editable state; archiving is its only outgoing transition.
Unlike
Pause
, Archive does not end agent sessions. It refuses while any session is still active or paused, so a session cannot be stranded on an archived Campaign.
Path params
id
String
Unique identifier for the Campaign (UUID).
code
description
404
No Campaign with this id in your company.
message
is
NOT_FOUND
.
409
The Campaign was modified concurrently by another request. Re-read the Campaign and retry.
message
is
CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION
.
422
The Campaign is not in
draft
,
paused
or
completed
status (
message: INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION
); or it has an agent session that is still active or paused (
message: UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY
).
Request
POST
/v1/campaigns/:id/archive

    "name": "Q3 outreach",

Retrieve Campaign statistics
Fetch progress numbers for a
Campaign
: how many contacts are done, how many remain, and how the calls that connected went.
These numbers are computed on read, not cached. Poll no more often than once a minute per Campaign.
Path params
id
String
Unique identifier for the Campaign (UUID).
Attributes
total_contacts
Integer
Contacts in the Campaign, queued or not.
completed_percentage
Float
Share of contacts that have gone through the Campaign, 0-100 with two decimal places.
0
when nothing has been queued yet.
connected_contacts
Integer
Contacts who answered at least one call.
average_call_duration
Integer
Average duration, in seconds, of calls that connected.
Can be
null
until at least one call has connected.
active_time
Integer
Total time, in seconds, the Campaign has spent in the
in_progress
status, across every pause and resume.
active_at
String
When the Campaign most recently entered
in_progress
, in UTC.
Can be
null
if it never has.
active_calls_count
Integer
Calls this Campaign has in progress right now.
code
description
404
No Campaign with this id in your company.

/v1/campaigns/:id/stats

Record the result
Manage the company-wide
Campaign Outcome
catalogue of custom disposition labels. Outcomes are either applied automatically by the system or logged manually by an agent during wrap-up. For calls placed on a specific
Campaign
,
list its call outcomes
to retrieve all recorded outcomes, including both system-applied and agent-provided results. An external system can also
record an outcome
through the API.
A Campaign Outcome is a reusable catalogue entry. A Campaign call outcome is the disposition recorded against one call attempt. The similar names describe different resources.
The Campaign Outcome object
A Campaign Outcome is a company-defined call disposition - a custom label an agent (or an external system, through the API) can attach to a
Campaign
call attempt to record how it went, alongside Aircall's own built-in outcomes like
voicemail
or
unanswered
.
Outcomes are defined once per company and reused across every Campaign - this is why the resource is company-scoped at
/v1/campaign_outcomes
rather than nested under a single Campaign.
This resource exists to complete a reference that already shipped:
List Campaign call outcomes
can return
outcome: { "type": "custom", "id": "...", "name": "...", "color": "..." }
for an attempt disposed with a custom outcome. This is that outcome's own catalogue entry, addressable by the same
id
.
An API token has
company-wide
access to Campaign Outcomes, the same as every other resource on this surface - a token can see, create, update and delete every outcome in the company.
Attributes
id
String
Unique identifier for the Campaign Outcome (UUID).
name
String
The outcome's display name.
color
String
Display colour chosen in the dashboard, as a hex string (e.g.
#ff0000
).
Can be
null
when unset.
created_at
String
Campaign Outcome's creation date, in UTC.
updated_at
String
Campaign Outcome's last update date, in UTC.
Endpoints

/v1/campaign_outcomes

Update a Campaign Outcome
DELETE
/v1/campaign_outcomes/:id
Delete a Campaign Outcome

/v1/campaign_outcomes
List all Campaign Outcomes

/v1/campaign_outcomes/:id

.

codes and hyphen/underscore variants) is rejected with a

. A name already used by another of your company's outcomes is also a

.

Update a Campaign Outcome
Rename a
Campaign Outcome
and/or change its colour.
Despite the
PUT
verb, the two fields behave differently from each other, and neither behaves like a full replacement. This API has no
PATCH
method, so
PUT
is reused here, asymmetrically:
field omitted from body
effect
name
400
-
name
is required on every request, there is no "leave the name alone".
color
Left unchanged.
color: null
Cleared.
color: "#rrggbb"
Set to the given value.
A caller round-tripping
GET
→
PUT
must be prepared to send
color: null
back for an outcome that has no colour, rather than omitting the key.
Path params
id
String
Unique identifier for the Campaign Outcome (UUID).
Body params
name
String
The outcome's display name.
Mandatory on every request. Cannot be blank. Max 255 characters.
color
String
Display colour, as a 6-digit hex string (e.g.
#ff0000
).
Optional. Omit to leave unchanged, send
null
to clear, or send a value to set it.
code
description
400
A body field is out of range, or carries an unknown key.
404
No Campaign Outcome with this id in your company.
409
The Campaign Outcome was modified concurrently by another request. Re-read it and retry.
message
is
CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION
.
422
The new name is reserved by a built-in outcome (
message: RESERVED_OUTCOME_NAME
), or already used by another outcome in your company (
message: OUTCOME_ALREADY_EXISTS
).
Request
PUT
/v1/campaign_outcomes/:id

  "name": "Callback requested - urgent",
  "color": "#ff9900"
}
Response
Status: 200 OK

{
  "campaign_outcome": {
    "id": "66666666-6666-4666-8666-666666666666",
    "name": "Callback requested - urgent",
    "color": "#ff9900",
    "created_at": "2026-07-15T10:00:00.000Z",
    "updated_at": "2026-07-20T08:30:00.000Z"
  }
}
Delete a Campaign Outcome
Soft-delete a
Campaign Outcome
. The row is marked deleted and disappears from every read path - the same effect as deleting from the dashboard. Its name becomes reusable by a new outcome immediately afterwards.
An outcome cannot be deleted while it is still linked to a non-deleted Campaign, or while at least one call attempt has already recorded it - even one on a Campaign this outcome is no longer linked to. There is no way to force it: unlink the Campaign(s) first, but a recorded attempt is permanent, so an outcome that has ever been used on a real call can never be deleted afterwards.
Path params
id
String
Unique identifier for the Campaign Outcome (UUID).
code
description
404
No Campaign Outcome with this id in your company.
409
The Campaign Outcome was modified concurrently by another request. Re-read it and retry.
message
is
CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION
.
422
The outcome is still linked to a Campaign and cannot be deleted; or it has been recorded on at least one call and cannot be deleted.
message
is
OUTCOME_IN_USE
.
Request
DELETE
/v1/campaign_outcomes/:id
Response
Status: 204 No Content
