Call AI Voice Agents
Call AI Voice Agents overview
The
ai_voice_agents
array on the
call object
lists the AI Voice Agent segments that handled a call — one entry per agent, ordered by start time. A single call may involve several segments when escalations are chained (for example Triage → Numbers).
These objects are read-only and cannot be updated or deleted via the Aircall Public API.
This is distinct from the
AI Voice Agent
webhook events, which report the AI Voice Agent lifecycle in real time; the attributes below are the summarized per-segment data attached to the Call object.
Attributes
agent_id
String
Unique identifier of the AI Voice Agent that handled the segment.
agent_name
String
Name of the AI Voice Agent.
end_reason
String
How the segment ended.
started_at
Integer
Timestamp when the segment started, in UTC.
ended_at
Integer
Timestamp when the segment ended, in UTC.
escalation_reason
String
Reason the segment was escalated.
null
when the segment did not escalate.
escalation_target
Object
Where the segment was escalated to.
null
when the segment did not escalate. Contains
type
and
destination
.
type
possible values:
branch
- inbound routing branch;
destination
is the branch name.
user
- outbound transfer to a user;
destination
is the user id.
team
- outbound transfer to a team;
destination
is the team id.
external
- outbound transfer to an external number;
destination
is that number.
Available when
fetch_aiva_conv
is set to “true” in the query parameter for
Retrieve a Call
,
List all Calls
, and
Search Calls
. Returns an empty array when no AI Voice Agent handled the call.
Trigger an Outbound Call
An Outbound Voice Agent places calls
to
contacts on behalf of your company. Instead of answering incoming calls, the agent proactively dials out — triggered by an external system like a CRM, scheduling tool, or automation platform via this endpoint.
Common use cases include appointment reminders, billing follow-ups, post-service surveys, and lead qualification.
Before using this endpoint, configure your
Outbound Voice Agent
in the Aircall Dashboard. The agent must have a connected phone number and a first message defined.
Outbound calls are subject to the limits below. Requests are queued until a slot is available; if
expiration_seconds
elapses before a slot opens, the call is dropped and never placed.
limit
value
notes
API rate limit
120 requests / minute / company
Standard Aircall
API rate limit
. Caps how fast requests can be submitted; accepted requests are queued and placed when concurrency allows.
Per-company concurrency
5 concurrent calls
Maximum outbound agent calls a single company can have in flight at any time.
Per-number concurrency
1 concurrent call
Each agent has a dedicated phone number (1:1); that number can only host one live call at a time.
Path params
agent_id
String
Unique identifier for the Outbound Voice Agent.
Can be found in the Outbound Call Workflow tab of the agent's configuration page.
Body params
contact_phone
String
The phone number to call,
with country code
. Leading
+
is optional. Spaces, dashes, and parentheses are stripped automatically.
Mandatory field. Examples:
+15551234567
,
15551234567
,
+1 (555) 123-4567
.
idempotency_key
String
A unique identifier for this call request. Sending the same key twice will not create a duplicate call.
Mandatory field. Use something meaningful like
campaign-{date}-{customer_id}
. A key cannot be reused even if the original request failed — generate a new key for each retry.
context
Object
Key-value pairs that fill in
{{variable}}
placeholders in the agent's first message, voicemail message, and conversation guidelines. See
Template Variables
for details.
Every
{{variable}}
in the first message and voicemail message
must
have a corresponding key — otherwise the call is rejected. Variables in the conversation guidelines are optional.
expiration_seconds
Integer
How long (in seconds) the system will keep trying to place the call if the concurrency limit is reached.
Min:
60
. Max:
86400
(24 hours). Default:
3600
(1 hour).
code
description
202
Call request accepted and queued.
400
Invalid request. Missing template variables, invalid phone number format, or other validation error. Check
error_message
in the response.
403
Outbound calling is not enabled for this company.
404
Agent not found. Verify the agent ID is correct.
409
Duplicate idempotency key, or agent does not have a phone number assigned.
422
Missing required field (
contact_phone
or
idempotency_key
).
500
Unexpected server error.
503
Internal service error. Retry with a new idempotency key.
Request
POST
/v1/outbound-calls/agents/:agent_id

{
  "contact_phone": "+15551234567",
  "idempotency_key": "appt-reminder-2024-03-15-cust-12345",
  "context": {
    "first_name": "Jane",
    "appointment_date": "March 20th at 2:00 PM"
  },
  "expiration_seconds": 3600
}
Response
Status: 202 Accepted

{
  "id": "01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FAV",
  "idempotency_key": "appt-reminder-2024-03-15-cust-12345",
  "status": "PENDING",
  "virtual_agent_id": "agent-abc123"
}
Template Variables
The
context
object in the
Trigger an Outbound Call
request provides values for any
{{variable}}
placeholders used in the agent's first message, voicemail message, or conversation guidelines.
location
variable type
if missing from
context
First message
Required
The call is rejected with
400
.
Voicemail message
Required
The call is rejected with
400
.
Conversation guidelines
Optional
The call is placed; the agent proceeds without that value.
Required variables:
Every
{{variable}}
in the first message and voicemail message
must
have a corresponding key in
context
. The value is substituted directly into the message before it is spoken.
Optional variables:
{{variable}}
placeholders in the conversation guidelines are not substituted as literal text. Instead, every key in
context
is shared with the agent as information about the contact, and the agent uses those values wherever the guidelines reference them. Use optional variables for details that improve the conversation but are not needed to start it, such as an account tier or a recent order. If an optional variable may not always be provided, write the guidelines so they still make sense without it (for example, "If you know the customer's last order, mention it.").
Keys in
context
that are not referenced by any template are still shared with the agent as information about the contact.
Variable names can only contain letters, numbers, and underscores — no spaces (e.g.
{{first_name}}
, not
{{first name}}
).
Values can be strings, numbers, or dates.
Datetime handling:
If a context value is a UTC datetime in ISO 8601 format (e.g.,
2024-03-20T14:30:00Z
), the system automatically converts it to natural language. In this case, a
timezone
key is
required
in the context so the system knows what local time to use.
If the datetime includes an offset (e.g.,
2024-03-20T10:30:00-04:00
), no
timezone
key is needed. You can also pass dates pre-formatted as plain strings.
Body params
context
Object
Key-value pairs that fill in
{{variable}}
placeholders.
context.timezone
String
IANA timezone identifier for datetime conversion.
Required when a context value is a UTC datetime (e.g.,
2024-03-20T14:30:00Z
). Not needed if datetime includes an offset or is pre-formatted. Example:
America/New_York
.
Request
POST
/v1/outbound-calls/agents/:agent_id

{
  "contact_phone": "+15551234567",
  "idempotency_key": "appt-reminder-2024-03-15-cust-12345",
  "context": {
    "first_name": "Jane",
    "appointment_date": "2024-03-20T14:30:00Z",
    "timezone": "America/New_York"
  }
}
Response
The agent will say: "March 20th at 10:30 AM"
(converted from UTC to Eastern time)
Event Statuses
Each
outbound call
request creates an event that progresses through these statuses:
Attributes
PENDING
String
The call request has been accepted and is queued.
INITIATED
String
The call has been placed and the phone is ringing.
IN_PROGRESS
String
Someone or something answered and the agent is actively on the call.
COMPLETED
String
The call has finished.
Call outcome (e.g., human answered, voicemail, no answer) is available in the notes section of the call in the Conversation Center.
FAILED
String
The call could not be completed due to network issues, timeouts, or repeated failed attempts.
On system failures, the call is automatically reattempted, provided the call did not fail mid-conversation.
REJECTED
String
The call was never attempted because the request was invalid.
Check
error_message
in the response for the specific reason.
Normal flow:
PENDING
→
INITIATED
→
IN_PROGRESS
→
COMPLETED
Outbound calls are logged in the Conversation Center like standard calls.
AI Voice Agent webhook events
are emitted for outbound calls with an additional
direction
field.
Analytics
Analytics overview
The Analytics API exposes Aircall's analytics data through the public API as
asynchronous exports
. Generating a report is a two-step flow:
Create an export job with
Create an Analytics Report Export
. The response returns an
exportID
and a
status
of
PENDING
.
Poll
Retrieve an Analytics Report Export
until the
status
becomes
COMPLETED
(or
FAILED
). When complete, the response includes a presigned
downloadUrl
you can use to fetch the file.
Twelve report types are supported, covering call history, user status history, and a range of user-activity breakdowns. The set of filters available is the same regardless of the report type; only the resulting file's columns differ. End-to-end completion typically takes seconds to a few minutes depending on the date range and report.
Exports are always scoped to the authenticated company — you cannot retrieve data for other companies.
The Analytics API requires the
Analytics+
entitlement on the company. Requests from companies without this entitlement are rejected with a
403
.
The
downloadUrl
returned on a completed export is a
presigned, time-limited URL
. Once
downloadUrlExpiresAt
has passed, the link stops working — re-call the retrieve endpoint to obtain a fresh URL while the export remains available.
Attributes
exportID
String (UUID)
Unique identifier for the export job.
Use this value to poll the export status.
createdAt
String (ISO 8601)
Timestamp when the export job was created, in UTC.
status
String
Current state of the export job.
One of
PENDING
,
COMPLETED
,
FAILED
.
errorMessage
String
Reason the export failed.
Only present when
status
is
FAILED
.
format
String
File format of the exported report.
Currently always
CSV
. Only present once the job has progressed past
PENDING
.
exportName
String
Caller-supplied name for the export, echoed back from
exportOptions.exportName
.
Only present once the job has progressed past
PENDING
.
isZipCompressed
Boolean
true
when the generated file is delivered as a ZIP archive (typically for larger exports).
Only present once the job has progressed past
PENDING
.
downloadUrl
String (URI)
Presigned URL to download the generated file.
Only present when
status
is
COMPLETED
. Expires at
downloadUrlExpiresAt
.
downloadUrlExpiresAt
String (ISO 8601)
Timestamp at which
downloadUrl
stops working, in UTC.
Only present when
status
is
COMPLETED
. Re-poll the retrieve endpoint to mint a fresh URL.
Supported report types
The value passed as
reportName
when creating an export selects the report's contents. All filters apply uniformly across report types.
report-name
contents
granularity
CALLS_HISTORY
Raw call log with timestamps, direction, duration, IVR path, participants, and tags.
One row per call.
USER_STATUS_HISTORY
Raw user-status events (Available, After-Call Work, paused, offline, …) with timestamps.
One row per status change.
USER_ACTIVITY_AVAILABILITY_BREAKDOWN
Time spent in each availability status, per user.
Aggregated per user / per breakdown period.
USER_ACTIVITY_AVERAGE_AFTER_CALL_WORK_TIME_PER_USER
Average After-Call Work (ACW) time, per user.
Aggregated per user / per breakdown period.
USER_ACTIVITY_AVERAGE_INBOUND_IN_CALL_TIME_PER_USER
Average in-call time on inbound calls, per user.
Aggregated per user / per breakdown period.
USER_ACTIVITY_AVERAGE_OUTBOUND_IN_CALL_TIME_PER_USER
Average in-call time on outbound calls, per user.
Aggregated per user / per breakdown period.
USER_ACTIVITY_CALL_VOLUME_AND_TIME
Call volume and total in-call time, per user.
Aggregated per user / per breakdown period.
USER_ACTIVITY_CALLS_HANDLED
Number of calls handled, per user, broken down by outcome.
Aggregated per user / per breakdown period.
USER_ACTIVITY_PERCENTAGE_OF_TIME_IN_EACH_STATUS
Share of time spent in each status, per user (percentage).
Aggregated per user / per breakdown period.
USER_ACTIVITY_RINGING_ATTEMPTS_PER_USER
Ringing attempts received, per user.
Aggregated per user / per breakdown period.
USER_ACTIVITY_WORK_TIME_AND_AVAILABILITY
Total work time and availability time, per user.
Aggregated per user / per breakdown period.
USER_ACTIVITY_RINGING_ATTEMPTS
Drilldown of ringing attempts across users.
Row-level.
Create an Analytics Report Export
Submit a new asynchronous export job for one of the supported
Analytics
report types. The response returns an
exportID
you can use to poll the job with
Retrieve an Analytics Report Export
.
The
filter
object is shared across all report types — only the resulting file's columns change with
reportName
. Provide exactly one of
filter.dateFilter.relative
or
filter.dateFilter.absoluteRange
to scope the date window. Exports are always scoped to the authenticated company; you cannot query data for other companies.
This endpoint requires the
Analytics+
entitlement. Requests from companies without this entitlement are rejected with
403
.
Instead of polling
Retrieve an Analytics Report Export
, you can subscribe to the
analytics.report_created
and
analytics.report_failed
webhook events to be notified when the job completes.
Date ranges are silently clipped
when they exceed retention boundaries — no error is returned. For
CALLS_HISTORY
, dates older than
6 months
are clipped to the boundary; for
USER_STATUS_HISTORY
, dates older than
7 days
are clipped. Always verify the date range covered by the output CSV before relying on the data.
Body params
input
Object
Wrapper for the export request payload.
Mandatory field.
input.reportName
String
Type of report to export. See
supported report types
.
Mandatory field. One of the 12 enum values.
input.filter
Object
Filters applied to the report data.
Mandatory field. See the filter table below.
input.options
Object
Report-specific options such as date breakdown and SLA configuration.
Optional. See the options table below.
input.exportOptions
Object
File-level options such as format and file name.
Optional. See the export options table below.
input.filter
Filters have different scope depending on the report type. The
notes
column flags applicability when a filter is not used by every report — these filters are accepted (no validation error) but silently ignored when set on a report that doesn't use them. Metric reports inherit the filters that apply to their underlying data (call-based filters for call-based metrics, status filters for status-based metrics).
Array filters always take an array, even when filtering on a single value:
"teamIDs": [12345]
, not
"teamID": 12345
.
Filter
dateFilter
Object
Date window the report covers.
Mandatory field. Provide
exactly one
of
relative
or
absoluteRange
.
dateFilter.relative
String
Pre-defined date window resolved against
timezone
.
One of
LAST_MONTH
,
LAST_WEEK
,
THIS_MONTH
,
THIS_WEEK
,
TODAY
,
YESTERDAY
. Mutually exclusive with
absoluteRange
.
dateFilter.absoluteRange
Object
Explicit date range.
Mutually exclusive with
relative
. Both
fromDate
and
toDate
are required when set.
dateFilter.absoluteRange.fromDate
String (date)
Inclusive start of the range,
YYYY-MM-DD
.
Example:
2026-01-01
.
dateFilter.absoluteRange.toDate
String (date)
Inclusive end of the range,
YYYY-MM-DD
.
Must be on or after
fromDate
.
timezone
String
IANA timezone used to resolve
dateFilter.relative
and to bucket date-breakdown rows.
Mandatory field. Example:
Europe/Paris
.
userIDs
Array<Integer>
Restrict the report to these user IDs.
Optional. Defaults to all users. Applies to all reports.
teamIDs
Array<Integer>
Restrict the report to these team IDs, interpreted via
teamFilterOption
.
Optional. Defaults to all teams. Applies to all reports.
teamFilterOption
String
How
teamIDs
should be interpreted.
Optional. One of
USERS_BELONG_TO_TEAM
(calls handled by current team members),
CALLS_ROUTED_TO_TEAM
(calls routed to the team queue, regardless of who picked up). Defaults to
USERS_BELONG_TO_TEAM
when
teamIDs
is set. Applies to all reports.
hours
Object
Filter calls by hour of day (
0
–
23
), evaluated in
timezone
.
Optional. Defaults to the full day (00:00–23:59). Applies to all reports.
hours.min
Integer
Earliest hour of day to include (inclusive,
0
–
23
).
hours.max
Integer
Latest hour of day to include (inclusive,
0
–
23
). Must be
>=
min
.
callIDs
Array<String>
Restrict the report to specific call IDs.
Optional. Applies to
CALLS_HISTORY
and call-based metric reports.
lineIDs
Array<Integer>
Restrict the report to specific number / line IDs.
Optional. Defaults to all Aircall numbers. Applies to
CALLS_HISTORY
and call-based metric reports.
customerNumbers
Array<String>
Restrict the report to calls involving these external numbers.
Optional. E.164 format. Applies to
CALLS_HISTORY
and call-based metric reports.
tagIDs
Array<Integer>
Aircall tag IDs to filter on.
Optional. Each value is a positive integer. Applies to
CALLS_HISTORY
and call-based metric reports.
callTypes
Array<Object>
Filter calls by direction and/or outcome. See the call-types table below.
Optional. Multiple entries are OR'd together. All inner fields are optional, but at least one should be set per entry to have any effect. Applies to
CALLS_HISTORY
and call-based metric reports.
duration
Object
Filter calls by total length in seconds.
Optional. At least one of
min
or
max
must be set when
duration
is present. Applies to
CALLS_HISTORY
and call-based metric reports.
duration.min
Integer
Minimum call length in seconds (inclusive).
Minimum
0
.
duration.max
Integer
Maximum call length in seconds.
Minimum
1
. Must be strictly greater than
min
when both are set.
minConnectedDuration
Integer
Minimum connected duration in seconds.
Optional. Defaults to
0
. Applies to
CALLS_HISTORY
and call-based metric reports.
ivrBranches
Array<String>
Restrict the report to calls that traversed these IVR branch IDs.
Optional. Defaults to all branches plus bypass. Applies to
CALLS_HISTORY
and call-based metric reports.
virtualVoiceAgentIDs
Array<String>
Restrict the report to calls handled by these AI Voice Agents.
Optional. Defaults to all voice agents. Applies to
CALLS_HISTORY
and call-based metric reports.
withAiIntervention
Boolean
If
true
, include only calls with an AI Voice Agent intervention; if
false
, exclude them.
Optional. When omitted, both true and false are included. Applies to
CALLS_HISTORY
and call-based metric reports.
withVoicemail
Boolean
If
true
, include only calls that left a voicemail; if
false
, exclude them.
Optional. When omitted, both true and false are included. Applies to
CALLS_HISTORY
and call-based metric reports.
ringAttemptOrigin
String
Origin of ringing attempts to include.
Optional. One of
ALL
,
EXTERNAL
,
INTERNAL
. Defaults to
ALL
. Applies to ringing-attempt metric reports only.
userStatuses
Array<String>
Restrict rows to these statuses. See the status values list below.
Optional. Defaults to all statuses. Applies to
USER_STATUS_HISTORY
and status-based metric reports.
nextUserStatuses
Array<String>
Restrict rows to those transitioning
into
these statuses. See the status values list below.
Optional. Defaults to all next-statuses. Applies to
USER_STATUS_HISTORY
and status-based metric reports.
input.filter.callTypes
items
Call types
direction
String
Call direction.
One of
INBOUND
,
OUTBOUND
.
outcomes
Array<String>
Call outcomes to include.
Each item is one of
ABANDONED
,
ANSWERED
,
ANSWERED_BY_AI
,
CONNECTED
,
CONNECTED_AI
,
NOT_CONNECTED
,
NOT_CONNECTED_AI
,
OUTSIDE_BUSINESS_HOURS
,
UNANSWERED
.

Array<String>
Unanswered-reason values to include.
Each item is one of
ABANDONED_BEFORE_10_SECONDS
,
ABANDONED_DURING_WELCOME_MESSAGE
,
ABANDONED_IN_IVR
,
ALL_AGENTS_UNAVAILABLE
,
CALLBACK_REQUEST
,
OUTSIDE_BUSINESS_HOURS
,
USERS_DID_NOT_ANSWER
.
Supported user status values for

and

AFTER_CALL_WORK
,

,
DO_NOT_DISTURB
,
DOING_BACK_OFFICE
,
IN_CALL
,
IN_TRAINING
,
OFFLINE
,
ON_A_BREAK
,
ON_MOBILE
,
OTHER
,
OUT_FOR_LUNCH
,
RINGING
.
input.options
Options
dateBreakdown
String
Bucket size for time-series breakdowns within the report.
Optional. One of
HOURLY
,
DAILY
,
WEEKLY
,
MONTHLY
. Defaults to
DAILY
. Applies to
USER_ACTIVITY_*
metric reports.
slaTimeToAnswerInSeconds
Integer
SLA threshold for time-to-answer, in seconds. Used by reports that compute SLA-based metrics.
Optional. Must be greater than
0
. Defaults to
30
. Applies to call-based metric reports.
slaIncludedCallUnansweredReasons
Array<String>
Unanswered-reason values that should count toward the SLA denominator.
Optional. Each item is one of

,
ABANDONED_DURING_WELCOME_MESSAGE
,
ABANDONED_IN_IVR
,
ALL_AGENTS_UNAVAILABLE
,
CALLBACK_REQUEST
,
OUTSIDE_BUSINESS_HOURS
,
USERS_DID_NOT_ANSWER
. Defaults to every unanswered reason except

(so out-of-hours unanswered calls do not count against your SLA by default). Applies to call-based metric reports.

Export options

.

String
Caller-supplied name for the export, echoed back on retrieve.
Optional. When omitted, auto-generated from

and a timestamp.
code
description
200
Export job created successfully.
400
Validation error. Common causes: missing

or

, both

and

set (or neither),

,

not greater than

.

Request
POST
/v1/analytics/report/export

{
  "input": {
    "reportName": "CALLS_HISTORY",
    "filter": {
      "dateFilter": {

          "fromDate": "2026-05-01",
          "toDate": "2026-05-15"

      },
      "timezone": "Europe/Paris",
      "teamIDs": [4242],
      "userIDs": [1001, 1002],
      "callTypes": [

          "direction": "INBOUND",
          "outcomes": ["ANSWERED", "ABANDONED"]
        }
      ],
      "duration": { "min": 30 },
      "withVoicemail": false
    },
    "options": {
      "dateBreakdown": "DAILY",
      "slaTimeToAnswerInSeconds": 20
    },
    "exportOptions": {
      "format": "CSV",
      "exportName": "calls-history-may-2026"
    }
  }
}
Response
Status: 200 OK

{
  "exportID": "9d3f2a1e-2b6c-4f0a-9c3e-1a2b3c4d5e6f",
  "createdAt": "2026-05-20T09:35:12.000Z",
  "status": "PENDING"
}
Retrieve an Analytics Report Export
Returns the current state of an
Analytics
report export job. While the job is processing, the response carries
status: PENDING
. Once it has finished, the response includes the final
status
and — on success — a presigned
downloadUrl
you can use to fetch the generated file.
Poll roughly
every minute
while
status
is
PENDING
. End-to-end completion typically takes seconds to a few minutes depending on the date range and report.
The
downloadUrl
is presigned and time-limited (currently ~1 hour from completion; check
downloadUrlExpiresAt
for the exact expiry). Once it has passed, the link stops working — re-call this endpoint to mint a fresh URL while the export is still available on Aircall's storage.
When
status
is
FAILED
, no data is generated and there is nothing to download. Read
errorMessage
before retrying — most failures are deterministic (invalid filters, unsupported combinations) and a retry with the same payload will fail again.
Once the job has completed, the response carries the download URL:
Response
Status: 200 OK

{
  "exportID": "9d3f2a1e-2b6c-4f0a-9c3e-1a2b3c4d5e6f",
  "createdAt": "2026-05-20T09:35:12.000Z",
  "status": "COMPLETED",
  "format": "CSV",
  "exportName": "calls-history-may-2026",
  "isZipCompressed": false,
  "downloadUrl": "https://aircall-analytics-exports.s3.amazonaws.com/...",
  "downloadUrlExpiresAt": "2026-05-20T10:35:12.000Z"
}
If the job failed,
status
is
FAILED
and
errorMessage
describes the reason:
Response
Status: 200 OK

{
  "exportID": "9d3f2a1e-2b6c-4f0a-9c3e-1a2b3c4d5e6f",
  "createdAt": "2026-05-20T09:35:12.000Z",
  "status": "FAILED",
  "errorMessage": "Report generation failed: too many rows for the requested date range"
}
Path params
export_id
String (UUID)
The ID of the export job to retrieve.
Returned by
Create an Analytics Report Export
.
code
description
200
Export job found. Inspect
status
to know whether to keep polling, download the file, or surface an error.
404
No export job exists for the given
export_id
, or the export has expired and is no longer available.
500
Unexpected server error.
Request
GET
/v1/analytics/report/export/:export_id
Response
Status: 200 OK

{
  "exportID": "9d3f2a1e-2b6c-4f0a-9c3e-1a2b3c4d5e6f",
  "createdAt": "2026-05-20T09:35:12.000Z",
  "status": "PENDING"
}
Webhooks
Webhooks
allow Aircall to provide and send real-time information to other applications.
Aircall Webhooks are designed to notify external system each time an event occurs on an Aircall account, like when a call is started, when a user is created and even when a contact is updated!
With that in mind, any developer can build automated tasks, specific to any businesses, based on these events.

user.deleted
user.connected
user.disconnected
user.opened
user.closed
user.wut_start
user.wut_end

user.wut_end.v2

outbound_campaign.status_changed
AI Messaging Agent's events
ai_messaging_agent.escalated
ai_messaging_agent.de_escalated
Click on an event's name to get more information on how and when it is triggered by Aircall!
Register an endpoint
The following steps describe how to register a server endpoint to start receiving Webhook events.
Up to
100 Webhooks
can be registered per account!
Login on your Aircall account (sign up
here
if you don't have one).
Go to the
Integrations' page
(
Integrations & API
section in Aircall Dashboard)
Search for
“Webhook”
and click on Integration
“Webhook”
in
“All Integrations”
section.
Create a Webhook Integration by clicking on the
“Install Integration”
button.
Provide a
name
and a
valid URL
and select all the events you want to subscribe to.
Click on the green
Add Webhook
button and… that's it!
Your Webhook URL must be behind a SSL certificate and start with
https
.
Aircall will start sending events to your server right after the Webhook creation, make sure your server is up and running.
Webhook usage
Make sure your server can receive
POST
requests, reads
JSON
format and always answers a
200
HTTP Code!
During development, you can create SSH tunnels to your local environment. At Aircall, we use and recommend
ngrok
which is free and open-source.
Web server
Once the endpoints are registered, a simple web server is needed in order to receive events.
Then, Aircall will send a
POST
request to the Webhook's URL each time an event occurs with a
JSON
payload body.
The Web server must be publicly available, Aircall does not provide a list of static IP addresses to whitelist.
Please note an event will be delivered at least once, if generated, but events might not be delivered in a specific sequence/order
Automatic deactivation
If webhook delivery fails, Aircall will retry the event up to
50
times. If the issue persists after all retries, the webhook will be automatically disabled and no further events will be sent. A notification will also appear on the Aircall Dashboard indicating that the webhook has been disabled.
Once disabled, Aircall will continue retrying the failed events for up to 12 hours. If a successful response is received for any of the failed events during this period, the webhook will be automatically re-enabled.
Admin users can receive email notifications when a webhook is disabled or re-enabled by turning on email notifications in User Settings within the Aircall Dashboard.
A request is considered
failed
if the server responds with an HTTP status code outside the
2xx
range, or if the request times out. To prevent your webhook from being deactivated, ensure your server always returns a
200
HTTP status code.
Aircall's HTTP requests sent to external web servers are set up to timeout after 5 seconds.
Filtering by Numbers
If the integration is built using the
OAuth
authentication method, Admins will be able to filter from which
Numbers
they want to receive Call events from on their Aircall Dashboard.
If the integration is using the
Basic Auth
method, Call webhook events will be sent for all
Numbers
of a Company.
Payload
Webhook events are sent with a JSON payload attached.
This payload mixes the JSON formatting of an Aircall's object (
User
,
Number
,
Call
,
Contact
) and some metadata like an absolute timestamp or the name of the event.
Webhook attributes
resource
String
Name of the resource for this event.
Can be
number
,
user
,
contact
or
call
.
event
String
Event's name for this payload.
See the complete list
here
.
timestamp
Integer
UNIX timestamp for when the payload was built, in UTC.
token
String
Token associated to the Webhook. Use it to identify from which Aircall account a Webhook event is sent from.
data
Object
Modelization of the
resource
at the
timestamp
value.
User
,
Number
,
Call
or
Contact
for more info on the data structure.
Payload
{
  "resource": "number",
  "event": "number.closed",
  "timestamp": 1585001020,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    "id": 456,
    "direct_link": "https://api.aircall.io/v1/numbers/123",
    "name": "My first Aircall Number",
    "digits": "+33 1 76 36 06 95",
    "country": "FR",
    "time_zone": "Europe/Paris",
    "open": false,
    "users": [
      {
        "id": 456,
        "direct_link": "https://api.aircall.io/v1/users/456",
        "name": "Madelaine Dupont",
        "email": "madelaine.dupont@aircall.io",
        "available": false,
        "language": "en-US"
      }
    ]
  }
}
User events V1
The following events refers to the
User
object.
Users can be invited by Aircall Admin users, from the Aircall Dashboard, or via the Public API (see
here
).
This version of User events V1 will be deprecated soon. Please migrate to
User events V2
.
user.created
Sent when new users are invited in an Aircall company.
Payload
{
  "event": "user.created",
  "resource": "user",
  "timestamp": 1585601319,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the User v1 object
  }
}
user.deleted
Sent when users are deleted from an Aircall company by Admin users.
Payload
{
  "event": "user.deleted",
  "resource": "user",
  "timestamp": 1589609314,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the User v1 object
  }
}
user.connected
Sent when users open Aircall Workspace.
Payload
{
  "event": "user.connected",
  "resource": "user",
  "timestamp": 158555000,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the User v1 object
  }
}
user.disconnected
Sent when users close Aircall Workspace.
Payload
{
  "event": "user.disconnected",
  "resource": "user",
  "timestamp": 1585603000,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the User v1 object
  }
}
user.opened
Sent when users become available according to their working hours.
Payload
{
  "event": "user.opened",
  "resource": "user",
  "timestamp": 158556187,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the User v1 object
  }
}
user.closed
Sent when users become unavailable according to their working hours.
For receiving substatus or unavailability reason selected by a user in Aircall Workspace in
user.closed
event, please reach out to Aircall support team at
support.aircall.io
to enable it for your company. Also, please note that this will result in two
user.closed
events being sent to you i.e. 1st one with just availability_status and 2nd one with substatus as selected by the user in Aircall Workspace. So please ensure to handle the two events at your end and not to treat them as duplicate events.
Payload
{
  "event": "user.closed",
  "resource": "user",
  "timestamp": 158554819,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the User v1 object
  }
}
user.wut_start
Sent when a user starts their wrap-up time (WUT) work according to their settings.
If wrap up time is set as zero and have mandatory call tagging enabled,
user.wut_start
event will be sent if user tags the call at the end of the call. However if call was tagged during the call(having mandatory call tagging enabled), the event would not be sent.
Payload
{
  "event": "user.wut_start",
  "resource": "user",
  "timestamp": 158554819,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the User v1 object
  }
}
user.wut_end
Sent when a user finishes their wrap-up time (WUT) work according to their settings.
If wrap up time is set as zero and have mandatory call tagging enabled,
user.wut_end
event will be sent if user tags the call at the end of the call. However if call was tagged during the call(having mandatory call tagging enabled), the event would not be sent.
Payload
{
  "event": "user.wut_end",
  "resource": "user",
  "timestamp": 158554819,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the User v1 object
  }
}

user.wut_start.v2
Sent when user start their wrap up time (WUT) work according to their setting.
If wrap up time is set as zero and have mandatory call tagging enabled,
user.wut_start
event will be sent if user tags the call at the end of the call. However if call was tagged during the call(having mandatory call tagging enabled), the event would not be sent.
Cold transferring a call to a teammate transfers the existing call leg and does not have any wrap-up time associated with it, so this event will not be generated for such calls. However, if a call is cold transferred to an internal Aircall number or an external line, this event will be generated as wrap-up time is required in these cases.
Payload
{
  "event": "user.wut_start.v2",
  "resource": "user",
  "timestamp": 158554819,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the User v2 object
  }
}
user.wut_end.v2
Sent when user finishes their wrap up time (WUT) work according to their setting.
If wrap up time is set as zero and have mandatory call tagging enabled,
user.wut_end
event will be sent if user tags the call at the end of the call. However if call was tagged during the call(having mandatory call tagging enabled), the event would not be sent.
Cold transferring a call to a teammate transfers the existing call leg and does not have any wrap-up time associated with it, so this event will not be generated for such calls. However, if a call is cold transferred to an internal Aircall number or an external line, this event will be generated as wrap-up time is required in these cases.
Payload
{
  "event": "user.wut_end.v2",
  "resource": "user",
  "timestamp": 158554819,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the User v2 object
  }
}
Call events
The following events refers to the
Call
object.
If your app is using the
OAuth
authentication method, Admins will be able to filter from which
Numbers
they want to receive Call events from on their Aircall Dashboard.
If your app is using the
Basic Auth
method, Call webhook events will be sent for all
Numbers
of a Company. You can implement this logic on your server!
Inbound calls
Here is a diagram showing when call events are being triggered during
inbound
calls:
Outbound calls
Here is a diagram showing when call events are being triggered during
outbound
calls:
Please note the following attributes have different naming conventions in Call APIs and Webhook events :
i.
sid
and
call_uuid
- Both refers to same value but in call APIs attribute is called
sid
and in case of call events its called
call_uuid
.
ii.
participants
and
conference_participants
- Both refers to same value but in call APIs attribute is called
conference_participants
and in case of call events its called
participants
.
Payload
{
  "resource": "call",
  "event": "call.created",
  "timestamp": 1732622896,
  "token": "cebcaac65XXXXXXXXXx",
  "data": {
    "id": 123,
    "direct_link": "https://api.aircall.io/v1/calls/123",
    "direction": "outbound",
    "call_uuid": "CAed058bba60a84d62c77cee898a852b05",
    "status": "initial",
    "missed_call_reason": null,
    "started_at": 1732622895,
    "answered_at": null,
    "ended_at": null,
    "duration": 0,
    "cost": "0",
    "hangup_cause": null,
    "voicemail": null,
    "voicemail_short_url": null,
    "recording": null,
    "recording_short_url": null,
    "asset": null,
    "raw_digits": "+1 800-123-4567",
    "participants":[
        {
           "phone_number" : "+1 800-123-4567"
            "type" : "external"
        },
        {
           "phone_number" : "+3512345678"
            "type" : "external"
        },
        {
            "id" : 123,
            "name": "John Doe",
            "type" : "user"
        }
    ],
    "user": {
     //the User Object
    },
    "contact": {
      //the Contact Object
    },
    "number": {
      //the Number Object
  }
}
}
call.created
Sent when a call is started.
For
inbound
calls, this event is sent when a call hits an Aircall number.
For
outbound
calls, this event is sent when Agents start a call from their Phone.
Please note in an IVR flow, the customer should link an Integration to either Parent or Child number in IVR Widget to receive only one call.created event. If Child number is linked to Integration then please reach out to Aircall Support team on
support.aircall.io
to inform and get IVR Call redirection enabled for Child number.
Linking both Parent and Child will result in duplicate call.created event generated for each number and should be handled by the customer at their end.
Payload
{
  "event": "call.created",
  "resource": "call",
  "timestamp": 1585001000,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the Call object
  }
}
call.ringing_on_agent
Sent when calls start ringing on Agents' Phone or on a Team. In case a call rings on a team, Team name along with other details will be included in the Teams attribute.
Payload
{
  "event": "call.ringing_on_agent",
  "resource": "call",
  "timestamp": 1585001010,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the Call object
  }
}
call.agent_declined
Sent when Agents decline
inbound
calls.
Payload
{
  "event": "call.agent_declined",
  "resource": "call",
  "timestamp": 1585001020,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the Call object
  }
}
call.answered
For
inbound
calls, this event is sent only when
one Agent
picks the call up.
For
outbound
calls, this event is sent only when the
external person
picks the call up.
The call
direction
is in the
data
object present in the payload.
Payload
{
  "event": "call.answered",
  "resource": "call",
  "timestamp": 1585001020,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the Call object
  }
}
call.transferred
Calls can be transferred either by Agents from their Aircall Workspace, or via the Public API (see
here
).
When calls are transferred to another
Agent
, this event is sent when the other agent answers the call.
When calls are transferred to a
Team
, this event is sent when one agent answers the call.
When calls are transferred to an
external number
via the Aircall Workspace, this event is
not sent
.
When calls are transferred to an
external number
via the Public API, this event is
sent
without the
transferred_to
field in the payload.
If the external party does not answer the call,
call.transferred
event will not be sent and call will return back to the original agent.
If a call is transferred to an internal number by dialling it in Aircall Workspace, then it will be considered as an external transfer and not as internal transfer. In this scenario,
call.external_transferred
event will be sent instead of
call.transferred
event.
More details on Transferring calls in our
Knowledge Base
.
Payload
{
  "event": "call.transferred",
  "resource": "call",
  "timestamp": 1585001030,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the Call object
  }
}
call.external_transferred
Calls can be transferred to an external number via Ring To widget in the Call Routing flow or by Agents from their Aircall Workspace.
When calls are transferred to an
external number
via the Aircall Workspace, this event is sent when the external agent answers the call.
When calls are transferred to an
external number
via the Aircall Workspace, this event is sent without the
transferred_to
field in the payload, instead it will have a new field
external_transferred_to
which will be a string indicating the external number.
When calls are transferred to an
external number
via Ring To widget in the Call Routing flow, this event will include a new field
external_caller_number
which will be a string indicating the caller's number.
In case of call transferred via Aircall Workspace, If the external party does not answer the call,
call.external_transferred
event will not be sent and call will return back to the original agent.
Payload
{
  "event": "call.external_transferred",
  "resource": "call",
  "timestamp": 1585001030,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the Call object
   "external_transferred_to": "919205152487", //New field
   "external_caller_number" : "+3253281310" //New field
  }
}
call.unsuccessful_transfer
Calls can be transferred either by Agents from their Aircall Workspace, or via the Public API (see
here
).
For calls that are unsuccessfully transferred to another
Agent
, this event is sent when the other agent doesn't answer the call.
For calls that are unsuccessfully transferred to a
Team
, this event is sent when no agent answers the call.
For calls that are unsuccessfully transferred to an
external number
via the Aircall Workspace, this event is
not sent
.
For calls that are unsuccessfully transferred to an
external number
via the Public API, this event is
sent
when the external line is busy and without the
transferred_to
field in the payload.
More details on Transferring calls in our
Knowledge Base
.
Payload
{
  "event": "call.unsuccessful_transfer",
  "resource": "call",
  "timestamp": 1585001030,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the Call object
  }
}
call.hungup
Sent immediately after calls are hung up.
Some fields present in the payload body might be null as Aircall needs ~30sec to gather extra data like the duration and recording files.
This event must only be used to know when a call is hung up whereas
call.ended
and
call.voicemail_left
can be used to retrieve extra data such as the recording file, call duration etc.
Payload
{
  "event": "call.hungup",
  "resource": "call",
  "timestamp": 1585001040,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the Call object
  }
}
call.ended
Sent ~30sec after calls are actually hung up as Aircall server needs time to gather and process extra data like duration and recording files.
This event must only be used to retrieve all calls information whereas
call.hungup
can be used to know in real time when a call is ended.
If the call or voicemail recording link is missing in call.ended event then the event
call.comm_assets_generated
can be referred/subscribed to, to receive the recording links when they are available.
Payload
{
  "event": "call.ended",
  "resource": "call",
  "timestamp": 1585002050,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the Call object
  }
}
call.voicemail_left
Sent ~30sec after calls end if a voicemail is present. Voicemail file will be included in the
data
object under the
voicemail
and
asset
fields.
Payload
{
  "event": "call.voicemail_left",
  "resource": "call",
  "timestamp": 1585002050,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the Call object
  }
}

ongoing

call.ivr_option_selected
Sent when a caller makes a selection in an IVR step within a Smartflow.
This includes both:
Keypad inputs in a traditional IVR (menu selection), and
Any caller input captured via the Input IVR widget (e.g., numeric input for account ID).
Use this event to track which routing path a caller chose during these IVR interactions.
Note: If the "Display caller’s input" setting is disabled in the Input IVR widget, the actual input value will not be included in the event payload.
Payload
{
  "event": "call.ivr_option_selected",
  "resource": "call",
  "timestamp": 1585001040,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the Call object
  }
}
call.comm_assets_generated
This event is sent when communication assets for a call i.e. call recording or voicemail is generated. Only one event per call is sent. This event can be referenced if call recording or voicemail link is not available in
call.ended
event.
This event will be sent only when a call is recorded or a voicemail is left.
Payload
{
  "event": "call.comm_assets_generated",
  "resource": "call",
  "timestamp": 1585001040,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the Call object
  }
}
Conversation events
Limited availability:
The Conversations endpoints and events are being rolled out gradually and aren't available to all Aircall accounts yet. If your account doesn't have access, requests to these endpoints won't work and the related webhook events won't be sent. To ask for access, please contact the
Aircall Support team
. We'll make this feature available to all accounts soon.
The following events refer to the
Conversation
object.
The
data
of each event is a Conversation, with one more attribute:
reason
. It tells why the event was sent.
Reason
reason
String
The cause of the event.
sms.received
,
sms.sent
,
mms.received
,
mms.sent
,
whatsapp.received
,
whatsapp.sent
,
whatsapp_username.received
,
whatsapp_username.sent
,
group_message.received
,
group_message.sent
,
call.received
,
call.sent
,
agent_request
,
api_request
,
bulk_api_request
,
support_bulk_close
or
auto_close_inactivity
. It is
null
when the cause is unknown.
The dates of the Conversation in
data
are ISO 8601 strings, while the
timestamp
of the event is a UNIX timestamp in seconds.
Delivery
Like every Aircall Webhook, Conversation events are delivered
at least once
. The same event can arrive more than once: Aircall can publish an event again after a timeout, and it can retry the delivery of an event to your server.
Treat the events as idempotent. To spot a duplicate, compare the
data.id
, the
event
and the
data.updated_at
of the events you already processed. The events have no event id of their own.
The events can also arrive out of order: a
conversation.closed
can reach your server before the
conversation.reopened
sent earlier for the same Conversation. Keep the event with the most recent
data.updated_at
, or call
Retrieve a Conversation
to read the current state.
Payload
{
  "resource": "conversation",
  "event": "conversation.created",
  "timestamp": 1790845964,
  "token": "cebcaac65XXXXXXXXXx",
  "data": {
    "id": 4321,
    "company_id": 123,
    "status": "opened",
    "number_id": 1234,
    "external_type": "phone_number",
    "labels": ["call", "inbox"],
    "assigned_to": null,
    "assigned_by": null,
    "assigned_at": null,
    "reason": "call.received",
    "created_at": "2026-10-01T09:12:44.000Z",
    "updated_at": "2026-10-01T09:12:44.000Z",
    "last_engagement_at": "2026-10-01T09:12:44.000Z",
    "closed_at": null
  }
}
conversation.closed
Sent when an opened Conversation is closed.
A Conversation can be closed by an agent in Aircall Workspace, with the
Close a Conversation
endpoint, or automatically after a period of inactivity. The
reason
attribute tells how:
agent_request
when an agent closes it in Workspace,
api_request
when it is closed with the API,
bulk_api_request
for a bulk close,
auto_close_inactivity
after inactivity, and
support_bulk_close
when closed by Aircall Support.
Use this event to react when the work on a Conversation is done, for example to create a ticket in your helpdesk. A full example is available in the
Conversation examples
.
Payload
{
  "event": "conversation.closed",
  "resource": "conversation",
  "timestamp": 1790847612,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the Conversation object
  }
}
conversation.created
Sent when a new Conversation is created, for example when a call or a message starts a Conversation between one of your Numbers and an external party.
Payload
{
  "event": "conversation.created",
  "resource": "conversation",
  "timestamp": 1790845964,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the Conversation object
  }
}
conversation.reopened
Sent when a closed Conversation is opened again.
A Conversation is reopened when a call or message is received or sent in it (
reason
is the channel event, for example
call.received
,
call.sent
or
sms.received
), when an agent reopens it in Aircall Workspace (
agent_request
), or when it is reopened with the
Reopen a Conversation
endpoint (
api_request
, or
bulk_api_request
for a bulk reopen).
Payload
{
  "event": "conversation.reopened",
  "resource": "conversation",
  "timestamp": 1790928331,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the Conversation object
  }
}
conversation.assigned
Sent when a Conversation is assigned to a
User
, or reassigned to another one.
The
assigned_to
attribute is the new assignee. The
assigned_by
attribute is the User who made the assignment, or
null
when the system made it. The
reason
attribute is always
null
for this event. Use the
Assign a Conversation
endpoint to assign a Conversation with the API.
Payload
{
  "event": "conversation.assigned",
  "resource": "conversation",
  "timestamp": 1790936445,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the Conversation object
  }
}

WhatsApp username message
The
whatsapp_username_message.*
events describe WhatsApp conversations identified by a Business-Scoped User ID (BSUID).
A BSUID identifies a WhatsApp user within a Meta business portfolio. It is not a phone number, username, or UUID.
You receive these events when:
You have created a webhook.
Your webhook subscribes to the relevant
whatsapp_username_message.*
events.
Aircall identifies the conversation by a BSUID because no phone number is available.
This typically applies to new contacts with a WhatsApp username and no prior phone-based exchange with your business.
Existing contacts can normally provide both a phone number and a BSUID, even with a WhatsApp username.
If a phone number is available, Aircall uses it and the conversation sends
message.*
events.
A subscription to username events does not change that routing.
See
Meta's phone-number rules
and
Twilio's WhatsApp identity documentation
.
Message
id
String
Identifier of the message.
Required.
external_user_id
String
BSUID of the external WhatsApp user.
Required.
external_user_name
String
WhatsApp username.
Optional.
external_user_display_name
String
WhatsApp profile name, distinct from the username.
Optional.
external_number
String
External phone number, only when a separate phone number is available.
Optional. Absent for BSUID-only identities.
body
String
Text of the message.
Optional. Sent and received events only. Can be absent for media-only or template messages.
created_at
String
Creation time of the message in ISO 8601 format.
Optional. Sent and received events only.
status
String
Message status as a lowercase string.
Required for received and status events. Absent from sent events.
sub_status
String
Reason for the status, when available.
Optional. Status events only.
type
String
Type of the message, for example
text
,
image
, or
template
.
Optional. Sent and received events only.
parent_id
String
Identifier of the message that this message replies to.
Optional. Sent and received events only.
origin
String
Origin of the message, for example
native
.
Optional.
delivery_method
String or null
Delivery method, when available.
Optional. Can be
null
.
metadata
Object
JSON object with information specific to the message type.
Optional. Not a JSON string.
template_content
Object
JSON object with the template content of a sent message.
Optional. Not a JSON string.
media_details
Array of Objects
Attached media files, each with
file_name
,
file_type
, and
presigned_url
.
Optional.
file_type
is a full MIME type, such as
image/jpeg
.
whatsapp_service_window_closing_time
String
Closing time of the WhatsApp customer service window, in ISO 8601 format.
Optional. Received events only.
whatsapp_free_entry_point_opportunity_closing_time
String
Closing time of the opportunity to open a free-entry-point window, in ISO 8601 format.
Optional. Received events only.
whatsapp_free_entry_point_closing_time
String
Closing time of the free-entry-point window in ISO 8601 format.
Optional. Status events only.
whatsapp_message_category
String
WhatsApp template category:
marketing
,
utility
, or
authentication
.
Optional. Status events only.
whatsapp_message_type
String
WhatsApp billing type:
service
,
regular
,
free_entry_point
, or
free_customer_service
.
Optional. Status events only.
service
identifies a free-form message without a template.
recipient_country
String
Two-letter country code for the recipient, for example
GB
.
Optional. Status events only.
number
Object
Number
object for the Aircall number.
Optional.
user
Object
User
object for the Aircall agent.
Optional. Sent and status events only.
contact
Object
Contact
object matched by a separate external phone number.
Optional. No phone-based lookup occurs for a BSUID-only identity.
Upstream serialization can omit default values.
These events do not include
channel
, media counts, or media sizes, and do not provide a BSUID-to-phone lookup.
The examples omit optional Number, User, and Contact objects.
whatsapp_username_message.sent
Sent when Aircall sends a WhatsApp message in a conversation identified by a BSUID.
The payload uses the
WhatsApp username message
fields and can include
Number
and
User
objects.
This event includes
created_at
in ISO 8601 format, when available, but does not include
status
.
Payload
{
  "resource": "message",
  "event": "whatsapp_username_message.sent",
  "timestamp": 1791531621,
  "token": "example-webhook-token",
  "data": {
    "id": "00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001",
    "external_user_id": "GB.ExampleUser123",
    "external_user_display_name": "Example Customer",
    "origin": "native",
    "body": "Hello, how can we help?",
    "created_at": "2026-10-09T07:40:20.661Z",
    "type": "text",
    "delivery_method": null
    // the Number object
    // the Contact object
  }
}
whatsapp_username_message.received
Sent when an Aircall number receives a WhatsApp message in a conversation identified by a BSUID.
The payload uses the
WhatsApp username message
fields and can include the
Number
object.
The
status
is
received
. This event includes
created_at
in ISO 8601 format, when available.
Payload
{
  "resource": "message",
  "event": "whatsapp_username_message.received",
  "timestamp": 1791531637,
  "token": "example-webhook-token",
  "data": {
    "id": "00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002",
    "external_user_id": "GB.ExampleUser123",
    "external_user_display_name": "Example Customer",
    "origin": "native",
    "body": "I have a question about my order.",
    "created_at": "2026-10-09T07:40:37.351Z",
    "status": "received",
    "type": "text",
    "metadata": {
      "type": "text"
    },
    "delivery_method": null,
    "whatsapp_service_window_closing_time": "2026-10-10T07:40:27.341Z"
    // the Number object
    // the Contact object
  }
}
whatsapp_username_message.status_updated
Sent when a WhatsApp message in a conversation identified by a BSUID has an updated delivery status.
The payload uses the
WhatsApp username message
fields and can include
Number
and
User
objects.
The required
status
is a lowercase string, such as
delivered
,
read
, or
undelivered
.
Twilio's
failed
and
undelivered
statuses both become
undelivered
, with an optional
sub_status
string that describes the reason.
Aircall skips updates without a status. It skips
sent
updates to avoid duplicate send notifications.
This payload does not include
created_at
or
updated_at
.
Payload
{
  "resource": "message",
  "event": "whatsapp_username_message.status_updated",
  "timestamp": 1791531628,
  "token": "example-webhook-token",
  "data": {
    "id": "00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001",
    "external_user_id": "GB.ExampleUser123",
    "external_user_display_name": "Example Customer",
    "origin": "native",
    "status": "delivered",
    "delivery_method": null,
    "whatsapp_message_type": "service",
    "recipient_country": "GB"
    // the Number object
    // the Contact object
  }
}

      "12025550147",
      "13105550293",
      "16465550812"
    ],
    "body": "test",
    "status": "sent",
    "created_at": "2026-03-03T09:35:00.520Z",
    "number": {
      // the Number object - the Aircall number that sent the message
    }
  }
}

      "13125550174",

Integration
object.

AI Voice Agent events
The following events refer to AI Voice Agent interactions and call handling.
AI Voice Agent Events are generated when calls are processed through Aircall's AI Voice Agent system, providing detailed information about voice agent interactions to help integrations better log and handle call information when AI Voice Agents are involved in the call flow.
Payload
{
  "resource": "ai_voice_agent",
  "event": "ai_voice_agent.started",
  "timestamp": 1585001000,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    "id": 123456789,
    "call_id": 123456789,
    "call_uuid": "CAxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx",
    "company_id": 1,
    "ai_voice_agent_id": "01XXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXx",
    "ai_voice_agent_session_id": "xxxxxxxx-xxxx-xxxx-xxxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx",
    "ai_voice_agent_name": "AI Voice Agent",
    "number_id": 123456,
    "external_caller_number": "+1234567890",
    "aircall_number": "+0987654321",
    "created_at": 1585001000,
    "started_at": 1585001000,
    "initial_direction": "outbound"
  }
}
ai_voice_agent.started
This event will be sent as soon as a call handled by AI Voice Agent has initiated/started.
This event is generated only if your Smartflow number has set up
AI Voice Agent
.
Payload
{
  "resource": "ai_voice_agent",
  "event": "ai_voice_agent.started",
  "timestamp": 1585001000,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the AI Voice Agent object
  }
}
ai_voice_agent.ended
This event will be sent when a call handled by AI Voice Agent has ended.
This event is generated only if your Smartflow number has set up
AI Voice Agent
.
Payload
{
  "resource": "ai_voice_agent",
  "event": "ai_voice_agent.ended",
  "timestamp": 1585001000,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the AI Voice Agent object
    "ended_at": 1585001030,
    "call_end_reason": "escalated",
    "initial_direction": "outbound"
  }
}
ai_voice_agent.escalated
This event will be sent when a call handled by AI Voice Agent has been escalated or transferred to a human agent.
This event is generated only if your Smartflow number has set up
AI Voice Agent
.
Payload
{
  "resource": "ai_voice_agent",
  "event": "ai_voice_agent.escalated",
  "timestamp": 1585001000,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the AI Voice Agent object
    "escalation_reason": "Caller requested to speak with support",
    "escalation_target": {
      "type": "branch",
      "destination": "Number Porting & Purchase"
    },
    "initial_direction": "inbound"
  }
}
ai_voice_agent.summary
This event will be sent on completion of a call handled by AI Voice Agent only if the agent successfully collected answers to intake questions configured on the agent (e.g., 'What is your full name?', 'What is your email address?').
This event is generated only if your Smartflow number has set up
AI Voice Agent
.
Payload
{
  "resource": "ai_voice_agent",
  "event": "ai_voice_agent.summary",
  "timestamp": 1585001000,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // the AI Voice Agent object
    "extracted_data": {
      "question": "what is your name"
      "answer": "John Doe"
    },
    "outbound_idempotency_key": "b3d7c1a2-4f5e-6a7b-8c9d-0e1f2a3b4c5d",
    "call_outcome": "HUMAN_ANSWERED",
    "initial_direction": "outbound"
  }
}
Analytics events
The following events refer to asynchronous
Analytics
report exports. Subscribe to them to be notified when an export job started via
Create an Analytics Report Export
finishes, instead of polling the
Retrieve an Analytics Report Export
endpoint.
Attributes
id
String (UUID)
Unique identifier for the export job.
Matches the
exportID
returned by
Create an Analytics Report Export
.
created_at
String (ISO 8601)
Timestamp when the export job was created, in UTC.
input_params
String (JSON)
Stringified JSON snapshot of the request parameters applied to this export.
Provided for debugging —
JSON.parse
to inspect. Treat the inner structure as opaque; the field set may evolve.
format
String
File format of the generated export.
Only present on
analytics.report_created
. Currently always
CSV
.
export_name
String
Name of the export (caller-supplied via
exportOptions.exportName
, or auto-generated).
Only present on
analytics.report_created
.
is_zip_compressed
Boolean
true
when the generated file is delivered as a ZIP archive (typically for larger exports).
Only present on
analytics.report_created
.
download_url
String (URI)
Presigned URL to download the generated file.
Only present on
analytics.report_created
. Expires at
download_url_expires_at
.
download_url_expires_at
String (ISO 8601)
Timestamp at which
download_url
stops working, in UTC.
Only present on
analytics.report_created
. Re-call
Retrieve an Analytics Report Export
with the same
id
to mint a fresh URL while the export remains available.
error_code
String
Machine-readable failure code, e.g.
EXPORT_QUERY_FAILED
.
Only present on
analytics.report_failed
.
error_message
String
Human-readable failure reason. Includes the export
id
as a reference for support tickets.
Only present on
analytics.report_failed
.
Payload
{
  "resource": "analytics",
  "event": "analytics.report_created",
  "timestamp": 1779730051,
  "token": "c779a2c9606c440f8adfb449654c7d8e",
  "data": {
    "id": "48c50ec9-751b-4f54-8790-c6881fe8de25",
    "created_at": "2026-05-25T17:27:30.610Z",
    "input_params": "{\"filter\":{...},\"options\":{...},\"dateRange\":{...},\"exportOptions\":{...}}",
    "format": "CSV",
    "export_name": "user-activity-availability-breakdown",
    "is_zip_compressed": false,
    "download_url": "https://aircall-analytics-exports.s3.amazonaws.com/exports/48c50ec9-751b-4f54-8790-c6881fe8de25/user-activity-availability-breakdown.csv?X-Amz-Signature=...",
    "download_url_expires_at": "2026-05-25T18:27:30.608Z"
  }
}
analytics.report_created
This event is sent when an
Analytics report export
job finishes successfully. The payload includes a presigned
download_url
for the generated file — subscribe to this event to skip the GET polling loop.
download_url
is presigned and time-limited (validity window indicated by
download_url_expires_at
). If the URL has expired by the time you process the event, call
Retrieve an Analytics Report Export
with the same
id
to mint a fresh URL while the export remains available.
Payload
{
  "resource": "analytics",
  "event": "analytics.report_created",
  "timestamp": 1779730051,
  "token": "c779a2c9606c440f8adfb449654c7d8e",
  "data": {
    "id": "48c50ec9-751b-4f54-8790-c6881fe8de25",
    "created_at": "2026-05-25T17:27:30.610Z",
    "input_params": "{\"filter\":{...},\"options\":{...},\"dateRange\":{...},\"exportOptions\":{...}}",
    "format": "CSV",
    "export_name": "user-activity-availability-breakdown",
    "is_zip_compressed": false,
    "download_url": "https://aircall-analytics-exports.s3.amazonaws.com/exports/48c50ec9-751b-4f54-8790-c6881fe8de25/user-activity-availability-breakdown.csv?X-Amz-Signature=...",
    "download_url_expires_at": "2026-05-25T18:27:30.608Z"
  }
}
analytics.report_failed
This event is sent when an
Analytics report export
job fails. The payload includes
error_code
(a stable machine-readable identifier such as
EXPORT_QUERY_FAILED
) and
error_message
(a human-readable description that includes the export
id
for support reference).
Failures are usually deterministic — read
error_message
before retrying, as a retry with the same payload will often produce the same outcome.
Payload
{
  "resource": "analytics",
  "event": "analytics.report_failed",
  "timestamp": 1779729574,
  "token": "c779a2c9606c440f8adfb449654c7d8e",
  "data": {
    "id": "38c8bb97-775d-484c-b0ac-0ed6cbb0658d",
    "created_at": "2026-05-25T17:19:30.334Z",
    "input_params": "{\"filter\":{...},\"options\":{...},\"dateRange\":{...},\"exportOptions\":{...}}",
    "error_code": "EXPORT_QUERY_FAILED",
    "error_message": "Failed to retrieve data for your export. Reference: 38c8bb97-775d-484c-b0ac-0ed6cbb0658d"
  }
}
Outbound Campaign events
The following events refer to
Campaigns
- Aircall's outbound dialler feature. Subscribe to them to react to a campaign's lifecycle changes and call outcomes being recorded, instead of polling
Retrieve a Campaign
or
List Campaign call outcomes
.
These events fire the same way whether the change was made from the Aircall app or the public API.
Attributes
id
String (UUID)
Attempt id.
Only present on
outbound_campaign.outcome_recorded
.
company_id
Integer
Company the campaign belongs to.
Present on every event.
campaign_id
String (UUID)
Campaign the event happened on.
Present on every event.
call_id
Integer
The call this outcome was recorded against.
Only present on
outbound_campaign.outcome_recorded
.
number_id
Integer
The Aircall number the call went through.
Only present on
outbound_campaign.outcome_recorded
.
attempt_id
String (UUID)
Id of the dial attempt the outcome was recorded for.
Only present on
outbound_campaign.outcome_recorded
.
outcome_id
String
The recorded outcome.
Only present on
outbound_campaign.outcome_recorded
. A UUID for a company-defined
Campaign Outcome
, or a fixed code (e.g.
do_not_call
) for a predefined one - never both.
outcome_label
String
Human-readable label for
outcome_id
at the time it was recorded.
Only present on
outbound_campaign.outcome_recorded
. Renaming the outcome later does not retroactively change past events.
attempt_number
Integer
Which attempt this was for the contact, starting at 1.
Only present on
outbound_campaign.outcome_recorded
.
max_attempts
Integer
The campaign's configured retry ceiling at the time of the attempt.
Only present on
outbound_campaign.outcome_recorded
.
call_uuid
String
The call's UUID, for cross-referencing with the
Call
object and its webhook events.
Only present on
outbound_campaign.outcome_recorded
.
agent_id
Integer
The agent who handled the call.
Only present on
outbound_campaign.outcome_recorded
.
agent_session_id
String
The agent's campaign session at the time of the call.
Only present on
outbound_campaign.outcome_recorded
.
status
String
The campaign's new status:
running
,
paused
,
ended
or
archived
.
Only present on
outbound_campaign.status_changed
.
previous_status
String
The campaign's status immediately before this change.
Only present on
outbound_campaign.status_changed
.
actor_user_id
Integer
The user who made the change.
Only present on
outbound_campaign.status_changed
. Absent for a system-driven transition (e.g. automatic completion when the queue empties) or a change made by a company-scoped API token with no acting user.
Payload
{
  "resource": "outbound_campaign",
  "event": "outbound_campaign.outcome_recorded",
  "timestamp": 1772355600,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // shape depends on the event - see each event below
  }
}
outbound_campaign.outcome_recorded
This event is sent when a call attempt within a campaign gets a disposition recorded - automatically for a predefined outcome, or via
Record a Campaign call outcome
for one applied after the fact. It is never sent for a call still in progress.
Payload
{
  "resource": "outbound_campaign",
  "event": "outbound_campaign.outcome_recorded",
  "timestamp": 1772355600,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    "id": "b84d1411-e145-56eb-c1g4-80bb740494bg",
    "company_id": 12345,
    "call_id": 12345,
    "number_id": 12345,
    "campaign_id": "a93e2300-d034-45da-b0f3-79aa639383af",
    "attempt_id": "b84d1411-e145-56eb-c1g4-80bb740494bg",
    "outcome_id": "c75e2522-f256-67fc-d2h5-91cc851505ch",
    "outcome_label": "Interested",
    "attempt_number": 2,
    "max_attempts": 5,
    "call_uuid": "CA88888888888888888888888888888888",
    "agent_id": 67890,
    "agent_session_id": "session-99"
  }
}
outbound_campaign.status_changed
This event is sent whenever a campaign moves to a new lifecycle status: launched (
running
), paused, resumed (back to
running
), ended (automatically, once its dial queue empties), or archived.
previous_status
is always included so you can tell a resume (
paused
→
running
) from a launch (
draft
→
running
) without keeping your own state.
Payload
{
  "resource": "outbound_campaign",
  "event": "outbound_campaign.status_changed",
  "timestamp": 1772355600,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    "company_id": 12345,
    "campaign_id": "a93e2300-d034-45da-b0f3-79aa639383af",
    "status": "running",
    "previous_status": "draft",
    "actor_user_id": 67890
  }
}
AI Messaging Agent events
The following events refer to escalation handoffs between Aircall's AI Messaging Agent and a human teammate on a messaging conversation.
AI Messaging Agent Events are generated when a conversation's escalation status changes, providing information about escalation handoffs to help integrations track when a conversation needs a human teammate's attention and when that escalation has been resolved.
These events are generated only if your line has AI Messaging Agent configured.
Attributes
id
String
Stable identifier for this escalation lifecycle event.
Present on every event.
external_number
String
The customer's phone number on this conversation, in international format.
Present on every event.
occurred_at
String (ISO 8601)
When the escalation or de-escalation actually happened.
Present on every event. Distinct from the top-level
timestamp
, which is when Aircall sent the webhook.
number
Number
The Aircall number the conversation is on.
Present on every event, when the number can be resolved.
Payload
{
  "resource": "ai_messaging_agent",
  "event": "ai_messaging_agent.escalated",
  "timestamp": 1758600000,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    // shape depends on the event - see each event below
  }
}
ai_messaging_agent.escalated
This event is sent when Aircall's AI Messaging Agent hands a conversation off to a human teammate.
Payload
{
  "resource": "ai_messaging_agent",
  "event": "ai_messaging_agent.escalated",
  "timestamp": 1758600000,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    "id": "01a0c324-4bd1-73af-a052-cff7899257ca",
    "external_number": "+33612345678",
    "occurred_at": "2026-09-23T09:12:44.000Z",
    "number": {
      "id": 48366,
      "direct_link": "https://api.aircall.io/v1/numbers/48366",
      "name": "Support Line",
      "digits": "+1 424-253-0150",
      "e164_digits": "+14242530150",
      "country": "US",
      "time_zone": "America/Chicago",
      "open": true,
      "availability_status": "open",
      "is_ivr": false,
      "live_recording_activated": true
    }
  }
}

This event is sent when a pending escalation on a conversation is resolved.
Payload

  "resource": "ai_messaging_agent",
  "event": "ai_messaging_agent.de_escalated",
  "timestamp": 1758601500,
  "token": "45XXYYZZa08",
  "data": {
    "id": "0a940458-ed31-42a7-bcb6-45c61f9062c1",
    "external_number": "+33612345678",
    "occurred_at": "2026-09-23T09:37:12.000Z",
    "number": {
      "id": 48366,
      "direct_link": "https://api.aircall.io/v1/numbers/48366",
      "name": "Support Line",
      "digits": "+1 424-253-0150",
      "e164_digits": "+14242530150",

      "open": true,

Mobile
In this section, you will find documentation for integrating with the Aircall mobile applications on Android and iOS.
URL Schemes
The
aircall://
URL scheme enables external applications to launch the Aircall mobile app. By using a formatted URL, any client application can hand off an action to Aircall by pre-filling the relevant information.
Open app (iOS)
Available since Aircall for iOS
v9.9.0
.
The
aircall://
URL scheme enables external applications to launch the Aircall iOS app directly. When triggered, it brings the Aircall app to the foreground, allowing users to quickly switch to Aircall from any third-party application without navigating through the home screen.
This is useful for CRM or helpdesk integrations that need to bring the Aircall app into focus before or after performing other actions.
Scheme Syntax
Parameters
This URL scheme does not accept any parameters. It simply opens the Aircall app.
Example
Opens the Aircall app:
aircall://
Behavior
If the Aircall app is installed and the user is logged in, the app will be brought to the foreground.
If the user is not authenticated, the app will open on the login screen.
If the Aircall app is not installed, iOS will not be able to open the URL and
UIApplication.shared.open
will return
false
in its completion handler.
Integration Example
// Swift
if let url = URL(string: "aircall://") {
    UIApplication.shared.open(url, options: [:]) { success in
        if !success {
            // Aircall app is not installed
        }
    }
}
You can check if the Aircall app is installed before attempting to open the URL by calling
UIApplication.shared.canOpenURL(url)
. Note that this requires adding
aircall
to the
LSApplicationQueriesSchemes
array in your app's
Info.plist
.
aircall://
Call (Android & iOS)
Available since Aircall for Android
v8.2.0
and iOS
v9.9.0
.
The
aircall://call
URL scheme allows external applications to trigger outbound calls from within the Aircall mobile app. By using a formatted URL, any client application can hand off a call to Aircall by pre-filling the dialpad.
Scheme Syntax
Parameters
Parameter
Required
Format
Description
to
Yes
E.164
The destination phone number to call (e.g.,
+14155552671
).
from
No
E.164
The Aircall line to place the call from. Defaults to the user's current line.
Phone numbers must be in
E.164 format
, which includes a leading
+
followed by the country code and subscriber number (e.g.,
+33612345678
). See
Dealing with phone numbers
for more details.
Examples
Basic call (default line):
Initiates a call to a US number using the user's default Aircall line:
aircall://call?to=+14155552671
Call with a specific line:
Initiates a call to a French number, placing it from a specific Aircall line:
aircall://call?to=+33612345678&from=+33140000001
Behavior
If the
from
parameter is provided, Aircall will attempt to place the call from that line. If the line does not belong to the authenticated user, the call falls back to the default line.
If the
from
parameter is omitted, Aircall uses the user's currently selected default line.
The app will prefill the Dialpad with the provided information.
If the Aircall app is not installed, the operating system will not be able to handle the URL and it will have no effect.
The user must be logged into the Aircall app and have an active session for the URL scheme to work. If the user is not authenticated, the app will prompt them to log in first.
Integration Examples
Android (Kotlin):
// Kotlin
val uri = Uri.parse("aircall://call?to=+33655445566&from=+33112233445")
val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, uri)
startActivity(intent)
Android (Java):
// Java
Uri uri = Uri.parse("aircall://call?to=+33655445566&from=+33112233445");
Intent intent = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, uri);
startActivity(intent);
iOS (Swift):
// Swift
if let url = URL(string: "aircall://call?to=+33655445566&from=+33112233445") {
    UIApplication.shared.open(url, options: [:]) { success in
        if !success {
            // Aircall app is not installed
        }
    }
}
On iOS, you can check if the Aircall app is installed before attempting to open the URL by calling
UIApplication.shared.canOpenURL(url)
. Note that this requires adding
aircall
to the
LSApplicationQueriesSchemes
array in your app's
Info.plist
.
aircall://call?to=
&from=

This changelog is here to present fields and endpoints that have been added overtime.
Versions
1.99.1
2026-11-11
The

value of the

attribute of the

object is now documented as

. The other values are unchanged.
1.99.0
2026-11-04
The
Campaign contact import
now imports directly: once the CSV is uploaded,

adds its valid rows to the Campaign as the file is read, and the create request accepts
insertion_point
(
start
/
end
, default
start
).
POST /v1/campaigns/:id/contact_imports/:import_id/promote
and
GET /v1/campaigns/:id/contact_imports/:import_id/rows
are removed - the retrieve endpoint carries the whole import summary - there is no review step, and the import
status
values are
pending
,
processing
,
imported
,
failed
and
stopped
(imports created before the change keep reporting
ready
,
promoting
and
promoted
).

0.0.1

🎉 Initial release.
Extras
In this section, we will describe a few non-essential things to know when working with Aircall Public API, like
Timezones
,
Phone numbers formats
and
emojis
.
CTI - Aircall Everywhere SDK
Looking to build a more advanced integration with Aircall?
Aircall Everywhere
is a JavaScript SDK that lets you embed and control the Workspace app in your website.
The Workspace app will be loaded in a HTML iframe and will send events to your main page when incoming calls are ringing, when calls are answered, when agents log in... You can also send instructions to the Workspace app such as dial a number or exit the keyboard! The full list of events is available on
GitHub
.
A demo website is available
here
and the full documentation of it is available on
GitHub
!
import AircallWorkspace from 'aircall-everywhere';

const aircallWorkspace = new AircallWorkspace({
  onLogin: settings => {
    console.log('phone loaded');
    doStuff();
  },
  onLogout: () => {},
  domToLoadWorkspace: '#workspace',
  integrationToLoad: 'zendesk',
  size: 'big'
});
Dealing with phone numbers
Aircall uses international phone number formatting in its Public API.
But dealing with phone number formatting can be complex and time consuming. We recommend using
libphonenumber
, built by Google, complete and up-to-date!
It extracts useful information from any phone number, like the number's type (mobile, toll-free, landline...), the country, the international/national/e164 format...
SIP trunking
Aircall uses several providers under the hood to buy and configure phone numbers all around the World.
As each provider is specific, Aircall does not offer SIP trunking capabilities or any advanced voice feature via its Public API yet!
The best way to get access to the audio data is to get the
recording
mp3 file URL at the end of a
Call
.
Timezones
Aircall dates and times are rendered in
UTC
.
Timezones listed in Aircall's products follow the
tz database
format.
Emojis
We love emojis, but they don't fit well everywhere in our products (yet!). You can display emojis in
Insight Cards
, to make caller insights displayed in Aircall Workspace more compelling 💅 . For the rest, the Public API will return an error if you try to update any string with an emoji 😱
